# rapaio-lib code review

Date: 2026-09-22. Scope: every package under `rapaio-lib/src/rapaio` (617 files, ~111k lines) and the
matching tests under `rapaio-lib/tst`. Analysis only; no code was changed.

How this was produced: the library was split into nine areas, each read by a reviewer and written up
with file:line references and a severity tag (`[bug]`, `[inconsistency]`, `[design]`, `[test]`, `[nit]`).
About 30 of the highest-impact `[bug]` claims were then re-checked by hand against the source; all held
except one reading of the `"?"`-level convention, which is discussed under theme 1.
The rest of the ~1000 findings have not been individually re-verified and should be read as strong
leads rather than confirmed facts. The `darray` package was reviewed separately on 2026-09-21 and
its findings were fixed in commit `7b46d10b`; it is not repeated here.

Finding counts by area (tagged bullets):

| area | bug | inconsistency | design | test | nit |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| core (stat, distributions, tests, tools) | 51 | 35 | 25 | 11 | 33 |
| data (Var, Frame, mapping, transform, ...) | 55 | 34 | 36 | 10 | 53 |
| ml.model trees / ensemble / boost (+ bayes, rule, knn, simple, meta) | 51 | 42 | 35 | 14 | 62 |
| ml.model bayes / rule / knn / simple / meta | 29 | 17 | 19 | 10 | 12 |
| ml.model linear / svm / rvm / km, ml.analysis (+ eval, loss, common) | 87 | 49 | 56 | 12 | 49 |
| ml.eval, ml.loss, ml.common | 38 | 23 | 22 | 6 | 27 |
| nn, math, ts, text | 48 | 34 | 39 | 12 | 35 |
| util, io, printer, sys, datasets, build | 56 | 30 | 32 | 15 | 49 |
| graphics | 78 | 24 | 35 | 17 | 93 |

Part 1 is the synthesis. Part 2 holds the full per-area reviews, unedited, with every file:line reference.

---

## Part 1. Synthesis

### 1.1 Verified bugs that change results silently

These were re-read in the source after the review. Each produces wrong numbers or wrong behaviour
without any exception, which is the most dangerous class for a statistics library.

**Evaluation and model selection** — FIXED 2026-09-22 (uncommitted at the time of writing; see 1.6)

- ~~`ml/eval/RegressionEvaluationResult.java:115` computes the cross-validation *test* scores from
  `split.trainDf()` and `trainResult`.~~ Reported test error equalled training error for every regression
  CV run. Fixed: test scores come from `split.testDf()`/`testResult`; the printed CV summary shows test
  (and, for reference, train) scores.
- ~~`ml/eval/split/Split.java:34` the convenience constructor fills train weights with `VarDouble.fill(n)`
  (zeros); `RegressionEval.java:172` / `ClassifierEvaluation.java:122` accept weights but never pass them
  to `fit`.~~ Fixed: unit weights; both evaluators pass `split.trainWeights()` to `fit` when present.
- ~~`core/SamplingTools.java:387-415` stratified split ignores the requested proportions;
  `trainTestSplit` default weights are the row indices (`:445`); `normalize()` mutates the caller's
  array.~~ Fixed: each stratum is cut at the cumulative proportions, default weights are 1, and
  `normalized()` returns a copy. Two existing tests that relied on the in-place mutation were adjusted.
- Also fixed in the same pass: `RegressionEval.run()` busy-spun on futures and swallowed fold failures;
  it now blocks on `invokeAll`, rethrows a failing fold as `IllegalStateException`, and clamps `threads`
  to at least 1.

**Models**

- ~~`ml/model/ensemble/BaggingMode.java:46` `HARD_VOTE` writes each density back unchanged~~ FIXED
  2026-09-22: votes are now counted (`+ 1`); the test asserts a non-first-class majority.
- `ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:165` `clamp_(1 - eps, eps)` has min and max swapped, so all kernel
  weights collapse to a constant: weighted kNN silently equals uniform kNN.
- `ml/model/bayes/nb/BernoulliEstimator.java:80` normalises on rows, storing P(class | x) instead of the
  likelihood P(x | class); the unit test asserts the wrong numbers.
- `ml/model/bayes/nb/MultinomialEstimator.java:170` `fit()` always returns `false`, so the estimator can
  never be used inside `NaiveBayes`.
- ~~`ml/analysis/LDA.java:205` drops the first class~~ FIXED 2026-09-22 (`"?"` audit).
- `ml/model/tree/CTree.java:259` depth stop is `node.depth > maxDepth` with root at depth 0: `maxDepth=1`
  yields a depth-2 tree and `newDecisionStump()` is not a stump. `RTree` has the same check.
- `ml/model/boost/GBTRegressionModel.java:128` fits the `initModel` *parameter object* itself;
  `newInstance()` copies the reference, so every GBT instance and every refit share one `L2Regression`.
  Lines 141-146 compute bootstrap weights and then drop them.
- ~~`ml/model/boost/AdaBoost.java:117,149` `k = levels.size() - 1` (`-Infinity` alphas on binary targets);
  `err == 0` gives `alpha = +Infinity`; prediction loops start at class 1.~~ FIXED 2026-09-22: `k` is the
  class count, `err` is clamped to `eps` before the SAMME weight, prediction covers every class.
- `ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:114,132` the meta-learner is fitted on density columns `1..K-1` of each
  weak learner but predicted with columns `0..K-1`: the feature sets differ between fit and predict.
- `ml/model/ensemble/RForest.java` inherits `rowSampler = RowSampler.identity()` from `RegressionModel`
  and never overrides it: a "random forest" does no bootstrap sampling unless the caller sets one.
- `ml/model/ensemble/CForest.java:206` `pvalue = normal.cdf(2 * normal.cdf(-|z|))` applies a CDF to a
  probability; permutation importance (`:284`) shuffles with the global `Random`, so `seed` does not
  reproduce it; OOB error (`:361-368`) counts never-out-of-bag rows (all-zero density → argmax 0, the
  first class) as errors.
- ~~`ml/model/rule/onerule/HolteBinning.java:83,133` indexes the frame by sorted position instead of
  `rows[j]`; `OneRule.java:143` loops `j = 1`.~~ FIXED 2026-09-22. The pinned iris expectation in
  `OneRuleTest` (petal-length rule, "1 error") was an artifact of the bug: that rule makes 7 errors; the
  new answer (petal-width, cuts 0.8/1.75, 6 errors) is the brute-force optimum.
- `ml/common/distance/MinkowskiDistance.java:39,54,63` `name()` returns "Euclidean" and `pow(delta, p)`
  has no `abs`, so odd `p` gives cancelling or NaN terms.
- `math/optimization/IRLSSolver.java:211,213` `w.div(w.sum()); w.mul(w);` are non-in-place calls whose
  results are discarded; the IRLS0M weights are never updated.
- `ml/model/svm/libsvm/Svm.java:496-501` the per-class weights are discarded: `weightLabel` is forced
  to `{1, -1}` with weights `cp`/`cn` regardless of what the caller configured. The `libsvm` package
  carries only the rapaio copyright header and no LIBSVM (Chang & Lin, BSD-3) attribution, which the
  BSD licence requires.
- `ml/analysis/PCA.java:69` the `center` parameter is registered under the display name `"scaling"`.

**Statistics**

- `core/tests/ChiSqConditionalIndependence.java:65-67` keeps `degrees`, `statistic`, `pValue` in
  `static` fields: every instance after the first reports the last computed values; not thread-safe.
- `core/distributions/Gamma.java:371-388` pdf/cdf/sampler treat `beta` as scale while `mean`/`var`
  treat it as rate, and `skewness = 2/sqrt(beta)` is wrong under either reading; tests enshrine it.
- Wrong closed forms: `Kurtosis.bigG2` denominator (`Kurtosis.java:74`), `Normal.entropy` missing 1/2
  (`Normal.java:128`), `LogNormal.skewness` (`:116`), `StudentT.var` negative for 1 < df < 2 (`:147`),
  `Hypergeometric.cdf/skewness/maxValue` plus int overflow, `KSTestTwoSamples` ECDF off by 1/n (`:65`),
  `ADTestGoodness.sigmaHat` missing `/n` (`:89-93`), `OnlineStat.max` initialised to 0.

**Data layer**

- `data/VarDouble.java:431` (and `VarInt`, `VarNominal`, `VarLong`) `removeRow` does not decrement
  `rows` when removing the last row; `VarInstant.removeRow` never shrinks. No test covers it.
- `data/finance/FinBarSize.java:38` `_15min` is defined as 115 minutes.

**Neural network**

- `nn/Tensor.java:208` `addGrad` stores the first incoming gradient by reference. Several nodes
  (`IdentityNode`, `Add/SubNode` when `sumTo` returns `this`, `Reshape/Stretch/CatNode`,
  `Sum1d/Mean1dNode` stride-0 views) hand back the parent's own gradient or a view of it, so a second
  accumulation into that leaf corrupts upstream gradients, including a user-set root gradient.
- `nn/tensors/DropoutNode.java:25` backward returns `grad * mask` without the `1/(1-p)` scale that the
  forward applies.
- `nn/loss/NegativeLogLikelihoodLoss.java:50` MEAN and SUM are inverted.
- `nn/optimizer/SGD.java:55-91` writes a plain `HashMap` from a thread pool and aliases the gradient
  array as its momentum buffer; both optimisers create a new thread pool per `step()`.

**Infrastructure**

- ~~`printer/Format.java` (all methods), `io/Csv.java:432`, `util/DoublePair.toString`: `new DecimalFormat()`
  uses the JVM default locale.~~ FIXED 2026-09-22, see 1.6. Also found while fixing: the default
  `DecimalFormat()` grouped thousands (`5,229.05884`), which several golden-string tests had pinned.
- `util/collection/Ints.java:250` `equals(a, aStart, b, bStart, len)` loops `i < len - aStart`.
- ~~`util/collection/IntOpenHashSet` / `Int2IntOpenHashMap`: cumulative quadratic probing on
  non-power-of-two tables can spin forever; `Integer.MIN_VALUE` is silently unstorable; `values()` drops it.~~
  FIXED 2026-09-22, see 1.6. Reproduced before fixing: both hangs, the `MIN_VALUE` losses, `/ by zero` on
  `allocation <= 1`, and an `ArrayIndexOutOfBounds` from `round * round` overflowing `int`.
- `io/Csv.java:242` `read` loops forever when `keepRows` rejects a row; `:389` `clean()` index bug.
- `graphics/plot/Plot.java:116` `this.options.bind(opts)` returns a new `GOpts` that is discarded:
  all plot-level options passed to the constructor are ignored. `Plot.yUnscale` (`:186`) adds `+ 1.`
  where `xUnscale` adds nothing.
- ~~`graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:291-294` casts `dmax / step * j` to `int`; for axes with a large
  offset and small range the cast overflows and the `start` loop never terminates.~~ FIXED 2026-09-22,
  see 1.6. Reproduced with `search(1e12, 1e12 + 1, 5)` and `search(-5e9, -5e9 + 2, 5)` (both > 3 s, killed).

### 1.2 Cross-cutting themes

These recur in several areas and are better fixed by a decision than by patching each instance.

1. **The migration away from a `"?"` level at index 0 was incomplete.** FIXED 2026-09-22, see 1.6.
   Commit `4eb48998` (2024-12-05) changed `VarNominal` so that `levels()` no longer contains `"?"`;
   missing is encoded as index `-1` and `Var.levels(true)` gives the old prefixed list. Verified by running
   `VarNominal.copy("a","b","?","c").levels()` → `[a, b, c]`. An audit of every `subList(1`, `= 1;` loop
   over levels, `size() - 1`, `emptyByLabels(...)`, `fromLabels(...)`, `DensityTable.empty(...)` and
   `levels(true)` site found eight leftovers that skipped or dropped the *first real class*:
   `LDA.java:205`, `AdaBoost.java:117` and its prediction loops, `OneRule.java:143`,
   `CTree.predictPoint` (`:345`), `DensityTable.hasColsWithMinimumCount` (`:292`, used by all three ctree
   searches), `HolteBinning` missing-rows histogram (`:131`), `ROC.from(score, actual, int index)`. All
   fixed. Sites checked and found correct: `OneHotEncoding` (uses `levels(true)` then strips),
   `Confusion` (`levels(true)` with `size - 1`), `DensityTable.empty(useFirst, ...)` callers (they pass
   `true`), `ChiSqConditionalIndependence` degrees, `NaiveBayes`/`Multinoulli`/`Bernoulli` estimators,
   `GBTClassifierModel.K`, `BinaryLogistic`, `BaggingMode`, libsvm `ModelInfo`. Reviewer text in Part 2
   that says "index 0 is `?`" describes the pre-migration convention.
2. **Sample weights are advertised but ignored.** `Capabilities` say weights are allowed in most models,
   yet `Search.Binary` (ctree), `Node.boostUpdate` (rtree), `GBTRegressionModel`, both evaluators,
   Naive Bayes estimators, L1/Huber minimisers, kNN, OneRule ignore them or use them partially.
   Decide per model and make `Capabilities` truthful.
3. **Randomness is not reproducible.** Global `Random` is used in `Splitter.Majority`
   (`Collections.shuffle`, on a list that is then unused), `CForest` permutation importance,
   `Var.shuffle()`; `RTree.learnNode` evaluates candidates in a `.parallel()` stream sharing one
   `Random`; `ThreadLocalRandom` and `new Random()` appear in several places. A model with a `seed`
   parameter should be bit-reproducible; today none of the ensemble models are.
4. **Missing-value semantics differ per `Var` type.** `VarDouble.getInt(NaN) == 0`,
   `VarLong.getDouble(missing) == 9.2e18`, `VarNominal.setDouble(NaN)` assigns level 0,
   `VarInstant.getDouble(missing)` throws NPE, `VarString.empty(n)` rows are not missing. Every generic
   algorithm that reads through `getDouble`/`isMissing` inherits a different rule per column type.
   A table of the intended contract belongs on `Var`, and each type should be made to match it.
5. **Type switches with holes.** `FLOAT`, `LONG`, `INSTANT` are missing from `AbstractVar.copy()`,
   `Var.deepEquals`, `refComparator`, `VSpot.compareTo`, `VarType.isNumeric`, `Unique.of`. Since Java
   21 `switch` over an enum can be exhaustive; making these switches exhaustive (no `default`) would
   let the compiler find every future hole.
6. **`newInstance()` shallow-copies parameter objects.** `ListParam.copyFrom` stores the same
   `Estimator`/`Prior` instances (`NaiveBayes`), `GBTRegressionModel` shares `initModel`, RVM/SVM share
   kernels with caches. A second model, or a refit, mutates the first. Parameters that hold state need
   a copy hook.
7. **Thread pools.** Created per call in `nn` optimisers, `AbstractFrame.toSummary`, `RegressionEval`,
   `Doubles.parallelQuickSort`; sized `availableProcessors() - 1`, which is 0 on a single-CPU container
   and throws. The shared, threshold-gated executor added to `DArrayManager` yesterday is the pattern
   to reuse.
8. **Locale.** FIXED 2026-09-22 for data output (see 1.6): `Format` builds every `DecimalFormat` from
   `Locale.ROOT` symbols with grouping off, `Csv.write` reuses those symbols, and the numeric
   `String.format` calls in `Format.pValue`, `DoublePair`, `KSTestTwoSamples`, `ClusterSilhouette`,
   `DensityVector` pass `Locale.ROOT`. Deliberately left locale dependent: `XWilkinson` axis tick labels
   (chart text for a human reader; `XWilkinsonTest.testLocale` asserts this behaviour).
9. **Silent coercion at public boundaries.** `VarBinary.fill(rows, 7)`, `VarRange` with a typo silently
   selecting nothing, `SolidFrame.byVars` truncating to the shortest var, `RidgeRegressionModel`
   accepting negative lambda, `BoundVar.from(counts, vars)` unvalidated. Prefer throwing
   `IllegalArgumentException` at construction.
10. **Four different exceptions for "unsupported operation"** across `Var` implementations
    (`OperationNotAvailableException`, `IllegalArgumentException`, `IllegalStateException`,
    `UnsupportedOperationException`). Pick one.
11. **Graphics2D state leaks.** Artists set colour/stroke/clip/alpha and do not restore; `fill == null`
    leads to `setColor(null)`, a no-op, so several artists render in whatever colour was last set.
    A `g2d.create()`/`dispose()` per artist in `Plot.paint` fixes the whole class.
12. **Build hygiene.** FIXED 2026-09-22, see 1.6. `--enable-preview` was on every compiler, surefire
    and javadoc configuration but no source used a preview feature (the shipped class files already had
    minor version 0, which is what the JVM checks); `requires java.sql` in `module-info` was there only
    because it transitively brought `java.logging`, which seven classes use; compiler, surefire, source,
    javadoc and jacoco config was copied into five child poms; `${argLine}` had no default so surefire's
    argument line depended on jacoco having run.

### 1.3 Design opinions

- **`Var` is a fat interface.** Every implementation must provide ~20 typed accessors it does not
  support. The type-specific surface (`getLabel`, `levels`, `getInstant`, `getLong`) would sit better
  on sub-interfaces (`NumericVar`, `NominalVar`, ...) with `Var` keeping the universal part
  (`size`, `isMissing`, `getDouble`, `copy`, `mapRows`). This also removes theme 10.
- **`Frame` row indirection through a shared mutable `Mapping`.** `MappedFrame.rvar()` hands the
  frame's mapping to each `MappedVar`, whose `removeRow`/`clearRows` mutate it. Mappings should be
  immutable values; mutation returns a new mapping.
- **Model parameter system.** `Param`/`ValueParam`/`ListParam` with self-typed generics
  (`M extends ClassifierModel<M, R, H>`) is heavy and leaks into every signature. It works, but
  `newInstance()` and `copyFrom` are where it breaks (theme 6). A `ParamSet` with a `deepCopy`
  contract per param would isolate the problem.
- **Two evaluation stacks.** `ClassifierEvaluation` and `RegressionEval` implement fold iteration,
  threading, result tables and printing separately, and diverge (one of them has the train/test bug).
  A single `CrossValidation<Model, Result>` engine with two metric plug-ins would halve the code and
  give both the same semantics.
- **ctree/rtree duplication.** `Search`, `Splitter`, `Node`, `Candidate` exist in both packages with
  the same structure and different bugs (min-count off-by-one asymmetric in one, `"?"` handling wrong
  in the other). The split search over a numeric column sorted once is identical apart from the score
  function; a shared search with a `SplitScore` strategy would fix both at once.
- **`nn` autograd ownership of gradient buffers.** The library needs a rule: a backward function
  either always returns a fresh buffer, or `addGrad` always copies on first store. Today it is neither,
  and the aliasing bug in 1.1 follows. Also missing: any finite-difference gradient check in tests.
  A generic `checkGrad(node)` test over all ~40 node types would have found every backward bug listed.
- **libsvm port.** Large, faithful port with correct attribution; but the port keeps libsvm's
  C-style shared mutable state (kernel caches, `svm_parameter` objects) which is not thread-safe and
  shared through `newInstance()`. Wrap the cache per fit.
- **Statistics tests assert the implementation, not the reference.** Several distribution and test
  suites hard-code the current output (Gamma skewness, Bernoulli estimator, `_15min`), so wrong formulas
  are locked in. Where a reference exists (R, scipy), the expected values should come from it, with a
  comment naming the command that produced them.
- **`graphics` option system.** `GOpt<?>` varargs with casts in getters is convenient but unchecked;
  the `bind` result being discarded in `Plot` went unnoticed because nothing type-checks that options
  reach their target. Image-comparison tests (90th percentile of pixel distance <= 45) cannot detect a
  missing line or a wrong colour; a handful of assertions on computed ranges, tick positions and
  palette indices would be more useful than more reference PNGs.
- **Documentation.** Javadoc is good on the big public types (`DArray`, `Var`, `Frame`) and largely
  absent on models, losses, optimisers and graphics artists. The `[nit]` lists in Part 2 hold the
  Javadoc-contradicts-code cases.

### 1.4 Test coverage gaps worth closing first

- `ml.loss`, `ml.common.distance`, `rapaio.text`, `core.param`: zero tests.
- `nn`: no gradient checks, no layer/loss/optimiser tests.
- `data`: no `removeRow` on the last row, no missing-value contract tests across types, no
  `MappedFrame` mutation tests.
- `ml.eval`: nothing asserts that test scores differ from train scores or that every row lands in
  exactly one fold.
- `ml.model`: kNN with k > 1 or ties, weighted fits, BINARY targets, unsorted data for OneRule,
  `newInstance()` independence.
- `util.collection`: hash structures with `Integer.MIN_VALUE`, non-power-of-two capacities, growth.
- `io`: CSV round-trip under a non-English default locale, quoting, `keepRows` rejecting rows.

### 1.5 Suggested order of work

1. ~~The evaluation bugs (`RegressionEvaluationResult`, `Split` weights) and `BaggingMode.HARD_VOTE`.~~
   Done, see 1.6.
2. `Tensor.addGrad` aliasing plus a finite-difference gradient test harness; then fix the backward
   formulas the harness reports.
3. The `"?"` level audit (theme 1) and the sample-weight audit (theme 2) across `ml.model`.
4. `removeRow`, the missing-value contract table, and exhaustive switches in `data`.
5. ~~Locale-fixed formatting~~, ~~build flags~~, ~~hash-structure fixes~~ (all done, see 1.6).
6. Statistics closed forms, with tests taken from R/scipy.
7. Design items (Var sub-interfaces, immutable Mapping, shared CV engine, ctree/rtree merge) as
   separate, larger changes.

### 1.6 Fix log

**2026-09-22 — evaluation path (step 1 of 1.5).** Source: `ml/eval/RegressionEvaluationResult.java`,
`ml/eval/RegressionEval.java`, `ml/eval/ClassifierEvaluation.java`, `ml/eval/split/Split.java`,
`core/SamplingTools.java`, `ml/model/ensemble/BaggingMode.java`. Tests added: `RegressionResultEvaluationTest`
(`testScoresComeFromTheTestFold`, `splitConvenienceConstructorUsesUnitWeights`, `trainWeightsArePassedToFit`,
`failingFoldIsReported`), `ClassifierModelEvaluationTest.trainWeightsArePassedToFit`,
`BaggingModeTest.votingMajorityIsNotFirstClass` (+ density assertions in `votingTest`),
`SamplingToolsTest` (`stratifiedSplitHonoursProportions`, `frequenciesAreNotMutated`,
`trainTestSplitDefaultWeightsAreOne`). Full `rapaio-lib` suite green: 2156 tests.

**2026-09-22 — `"?"` level migration audit (step 2 of 1.5).** Source: `core/tools/DensityTable.java`
(`hasColsWithMinimumCount` sums all columns), `ml/model/tree/CTree.java` (fallback density loop from 0),
`ml/model/rule/OneRule.java` (density loop from 0), `ml/model/rule/onerule/HolteBinning.java` (labels and
weights read through `rows[j]`; missing-rows histogram indexed by level, skipping missing targets),
`ml/analysis/LDA.java` (all levels), `ml/model/boost/AdaBoost.java` (`k = levels.size()`, `err` clamped to
`eps`, prediction from class 0 with `-Infinity` start), `ml/eval/metric/ROC.java` (`int index` is 0-based
over `levels()`; the two callers in tests adjusted). Tests: new `tst/rapaio/ml/model/NominalLevelContractTest`
(levels contract, `DensityTable` min-count on first class, CTree fallback density, OneRule first-class
density and sorted/unsorted equality on iris, binary AdaBoost finite densities, LDA separates setosa on
the first axis, ROC index vs label); `OneRuleTest.testSummary` expectation corrected as described in 1.1.
Full `rapaio-lib` suite green: 2163 tests.

**2026-09-22 — locale independent number formatting (part of step 5 of 1.5).** Source:
`printer/Format.java` (new `SYMBOLS` = `DecimalFormatSymbols.getInstance(Locale.ROOT)` and
`decimal(min, max)` factory with grouping off; all six formatters use it; `pValue` passes `Locale.ROOT`),
`io/Csv.java` (`write` uses `Format.SYMBOLS`), `util/DoublePair.java` (`toString` also had a double
`format`/`formatted` call), `core/tests/KSTestTwoSamples.java`, `ml/eval/ClusterSilhouette.java`,
`core/tools/DensityVector.java` (`String.format(Locale.ROOT, ...)`). Tests: new
`tst/rapaio/printer/FormatLocaleTest` runs under `Locale.GERMANY` and checks decimal point, no grouping,
parse-back and a CSV round trip (verified to fail against the old code). Golden strings that had pinned
grouped output were regenerated: `DArrayTest.testPrinting`, `TextTableDataTest.testFloat`,
`KMClusterTest.predictTest`, `KMeansResultTest.printTest`, `OneRuleTest` (`total=3528`).
`XWilkinsonTest.testLocale` now restores the default locale in a `finally` block; before, a failure in
that test leaked `ru-RU` into every later test of the JVM. Full `rapaio-lib` suite green: 2162 tests.

**2026-09-22 — build flags and pom deduplication (part of step 5 of 1.5).** `pom.xml` (parent) now carries
a `<pluginManagement>` block with the single copy of the compiler, surefire, source, javadoc and jacoco
configuration; `--enable-preview` is gone everywhere; the Vector API incubator module is added through one
property (`jvm.add.modules`), and `argLine` has an empty default so surefire works with or without
jacoco's `prepare-agent`. The javadoc plugin's contradictory `<additionalOptions>` (declared twice) is
replaced by `<doclint>none</doclint>` plus the module option. Child poms (`rapaio-lib`, `rapaio-datasets`,
`rapaio-experiment`, `rapaio-code-gen`, `rapaio-jmh`) keep only bare plugin references plus what is really
module specific (`rapaio-lib`: the mockito inline agent on `argLine`, the `dependency:properties` goal that
resolves it, the release plugin; `rapaio-jmh`: shade). `rapaio-lib/src/module-info.java`: `requires java.sql`
→ `requires java.logging` (the actual dependency; removing `java.sql` alone made seven classes fail to
compile, which is how the real requirement surfaced). Verified: `mvn test` on `rapaio-lib` with jacoco
active (2162 tests green, `jacoco.exec` written), `mvn -DskipTests package` on the whole reactor (javadoc
and source jars produced), compiled classes have class-file minor version 0. Net: −199 lines of pom.

**2026-09-22 — `IntOpenHashSet` / `Int2IntOpenHashMap` (step 5 of 1.5, last item).** Both rewritten
internally with the same public API: capacity is always a power of two; `QUADRATIC` probing is triangular
(`h, h+1, h+3, h+6, ...`), which is a full permutation of a power-of-two table, and `LINEAR` is sequential,
so every probe sequence terminates; the load factor keeps at least one slot empty and is validated to
`(0, 1)`; growth doubles from a computed initial capacity, so `allocation` 0..3 works. The set stores
`Integer.MIN_VALUE` (the empty-slot marker) through a separate flag, so every int is a member candidate;
the map still rejects `MIN_VALUE` as a key (documented) but keeps it as a value, and `values()` decides
presence by the key slot. `keySet()` sizes the set by `size` (was `size * loadFactor`). Tests: new
`tst/rapaio/util/OpenHashRegressionTest` (9 executions: termination at load factor 0.95 for both probings
under a 10 s timeout, `MIN_VALUE` as set member and as map value, allocations 0..3, invalid constructor
arguments, 20k-operation randomized comparison with `java.util.HashSet`/`HashMap`); run against the old
classes it fails 8 of 9, including two timeouts and the `ArrayIndexOutOfBounds` at probe index −14. Full
`rapaio-lib` suite green: 2171 tests.

**2026-09-22 — `XWilkinson` tick search overflow.** `graphics/base/XWilkinson.java`: the start bounds
`floor(dmax / step - (k - 1)) * j` and `ceil(dmin / step) * j` are now computed in `long`, and the loop is
skipped when the bounds differ by more than `MAX_START_CANDIDATES` (2^24). The number of candidates is
mathematically at most `j * k`, so a larger gap can only be arithmetic overflow from a step negligible next
to the data magnitude, where no candidate can score. Both hanging inputs now return sensible ticks in under
a millisecond (`1e12 .. 1e12+1` → step 0.2, 6 labels); all previously working inputs give identical labels,
and the 26 graphics image-comparison tests are unchanged. Test: `XWilkinsonTest.searchTerminatesForLargeOffsetSmallRange`
under a 5 s timeout. The other `XWilkinson` items from Part 2 (`simplicity` off-by-one, the `Q` set, the
`z == -1` case) are tick-quality questions, not hangs, and are left as listed. Full `rapaio-lib` suite green:
2172 tests.

Observed while writing the tests, not fixed, already listed in Part 2: `VarDouble.seq(n)` returns
`n + 1` values (`0..n` inclusive) while `VarNominal.from(n, ...)` returns `n`, and `SolidFrame.byVars`
silently truncates to the shortest var while the longer var keeps its extra rows (theme 9).

re-verified; the rest were not individually re-checked.


### 2.1 core (stat, correlation, distributions, tests, tools, SamplingTools)

# Review: rapaio-lib `rapaio.core` (src + tst)

Root: `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib`. Paths below are relative to `src/rapaio/core/` unless absolute.

#### rapaio.core.stat

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Kurtosis.java:74` — G2 denominator is `(n - 2) * n - 3` = (n+1)(n-3); the standard sample-excess-kurtosis formula needs `(n - 2) * (n - 3)`. Value is wrong for every n. [bug] Fix: `((n + 1) * g2 + 6) * (n - 1) / ((n - 2) * (n - 3))`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/OnlineStat.java:68` — `clean()` initialises `max = 0` instead of `Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY`; `max()` returns 0 for all-negative data. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/OnlineStat.java:144-176` — `update(OnlineStat)` merges moments and min/max but never merges `sum`; `sum()` is wrong after a merge. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/OnlineStat.java:112-118` — `mean()` returns 0 for an empty stat but `sum()` returns NaN; `variance()`/`skewness()` return NaN via 0/0. Empty-state semantics differ per accessor. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/OnlineStat.java:81` — `update(double)` does not skip NaN/missing while `WeightedOnlineStat.update` silently ignores non-finite input. Sibling classes disagree on NaN handling. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Quantiles.java:85-88` — when all values are missing the method returns an array of length `var.size()` (not `percentiles.length`), so `values().length != percentiles.length` and `toString()` indexes `percentiles[i]` out of range when `var.size() > percentiles.length`. [bug] Fix: `Doubles.newFill(percentiles.length, Double.NaN)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Quantiles.java:89-91` — with exactly one complete value every quantile is reported as `0` instead of that value. [bug] Fix: fill with `x[0]`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Quantiles.java:96-119` — no validation that percentiles lie in [0,1]; `p > 1` or `p < 0` produces `ArrayIndexOutOfBounds` (R7) or silently clamps (R8). Also uses `if/if` on enum instead of `switch`, so a future enum constant silently yields 0. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Quantiles.java:69` — stores caller's `percentiles` array without copying; caller mutation changes `toString()`/`toContent()` output. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Quantiles.java:52` — parameters are called "percentiles" but must be probabilities in [0,1]; R/numpy call these `probs`/`q`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Variance.java:66-69` — on `completeCount == 0` sets NaN but does not `return`, then recomputes (result happens to be NaN via 0/0). Also `completeCount == 1` gives `0/0 = NaN` for unbiased and `0` for biased, undocumented. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Variance.java:76` — `Math.pow(x, 2)` in hot loop; `d*d` is faster and exact. Same in `Skewness.java:67-68`, `Kurtosis.java:65-66`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Variance.java:40` vs `Mean.java:38` — `Mean`, `WeightedMean` are `final`, all other stat classes are not; `Variance.value` is non-final and assigned in a `compute()` helper while `Mean.value` is final. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Skewness.java:79-81` — `value()` returns `b1` (the biased "Minitab" form) while most libraries (R e1071 type 3 default, scipy default `bias=True`) return `g1`; `OnlineStat.skewness()` returns `g1`. The default differs between the two skewness implementations in the same package. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Kurtosis.java:77` — `value()` returns `b2` while `OnlineStat.kurtosis()` returns `g2`; same issue as skewness. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Kurtosis.java:46` — field named `G2` (upper case) while the sibling `Skewness` uses `bigG1`; accessor is `bigG2()`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Mean.java:44` — `Mean.of(double[], start, end)` exists but no `Variance.of(double[])`; `Covariance` needs the array form of `Mean` but computes variance-like sums by hand. API shape differs between siblings (Var only vs Var + array). [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Mean.java:79` — array constructor sets `varName = "?"`; printed output then says `mean[?]`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Covariance.java:56` — silently uses `min(x.size(), y.size())` when sizes differ, while `WeightedMean` throws `IllegalArgumentException` for the same situation. [inconsistency] Fix: throw.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/GeometricMean.java:67` — `var.getDouble(i) < 0` is evaluated also for missing rows (NaN compares false so it is harmless, but reads the value twice); `isDefined()` returns true when there are zero complete values although `value()` is NaN. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/GeometricMean.java:65` — value `0` gives `log(0) = -Inf` and result `0`; documented? Not in Javadoc. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/WeightedMean.java:62-125` — two `compute` overloads are byte-for-byte duplicates apart from the accessor; the Frame variant should delegate to `compute(df.rvar(varName), weights)`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/WeightedMean.java:79` — negative weights are accepted; `total == 0` returns NaN but negative total is not detected. `WeightedOnlineStat` rejects `w <= 0`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/WeightedOnlineStat.java:63` — `variance()` is the frequency-weighted biased variance `S/wsum`; no unbiased/reliability-weighted variant and no Javadoc stating which one it is. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/WeightedOnlineStat.java:31,48` — both a public constructor and a static `empty()` factory; `OnlineStat` has a private constructor and only `empty()`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/OnlineStat.java:52` — count `n` stored as `double` and exposed as `double n()`; `WeightedOnlineStat.count()` is `int`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/OnlineStat.java:148` — `update(OnlineStat)` allocates a temporary `OnlineStat` and copies back; also uses `+=` on a fresh zero (`combined.n += ...`). [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/stat/Sum.java:37` — uses Neumaier compensated summation while `Mean` uses a two-pass correction and `Variance` a naive `pow` loop; numerical strategy is not shared. [design]
- All `Printable` stat classes (`Mean`, `Variance`, ...) implement `toContent`/`toFullContent`/`toSummary` identically; an abstract base or default methods in `Printable` would remove ~15 lines per class. [design]

#### rapaio.core.correlation

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/CorrPearson.java:82-99` vs `CorrSpearman.java:61-69` — Pearson does pairwise deletion of missing values (per pair of variables), Spearman does listwise deletion (a row missing in any variable is dropped for all pairs). For >2 variables the two matrices are computed on different subsets. [inconsistency] Fix: document or align (R `cor(use=...)` semantics).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/AbstractCorrelation.java:45-53` — constructor rejects unequal sizes and then computes `rowCount` as the min of sizes anyway (dead defensive code); error message says "only between two variables" although N variables are supported. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/CorrPearson.java:85` — `min(x.size(), y.size())` is redundant after the base-class size check. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/CorrPearson.java:90-93` — builds four `mapRows` views and calls `Mean`/`Variance` twice each, then loops again; three passes over the data per pair. Compute sums in one pass. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/CorrPearson.java:93` — local `stateDependentParameter` is a misleading name for `sd(x)*sd(y)`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/CorrSpearman.java:119` — average rank for ties `1 + (start+end)/2.` is correct, but ranks are stored in fresh `VarDouble`s and then Pearson re-filters missing values that cannot exist; fine but wasteful. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/CorrSpearman.java:59` — lower triangle of `distanceMatrix` is set via `dp.get(i,j)` for all i,j although `DistanceMatrix` is symmetric storage; Pearson only sets the upper triangle. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/AbstractCorrelation.java:71-104` — `toString()` truncation counter `pos = 10` counts cells across rows and prints `...` inside a row; output for 4+ variables is hard to read. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/Correlation.java:32` — a correlation is stored in a `DistanceMatrix`; correlation is a similarity, not a distance. Type name leaks into the public API (`matrix()` returns `DistanceMatrix`). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/correlation/CorrPearson.java:54` — no Kendall tau, no p-value/CI; the package only offers point estimates. [design]

#### rapaio.core.param

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/ListParam.java:103-111` — in `hasDefaultValue()` the `ParametricEquals` branch falls through to `defaultValues.get(i).equals(values.get(i))` even when `equalOnParams` returned true, so a parametric-equal default is reported as non-default. `MultiParam` uses `else`. [bug] Fix: `continue` after the parametric check.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/ListParam.java:59-66,69-77` — `set()` calls `clear()` before `validate()`, so a failed validation leaves the parameter empty (state corrupted), and `validate` receives the already-cleared list as "old values". [bug] Fix: validate first, then clear+add.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/MultiParam.java:66-80` and `MultiListParam.java:69-84` — `set`/`add` never call `validate()`; the validator passed in the constructor is dead. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/MultiParam.java:39` and `MultiListParam.java:41` — identical `serialVersionUID = 6971154885891319057L` in two different classes (copy-paste). [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/MultiParam.java:41` — `TreeMap<K, T>` with unconstrained `K` throws `ClassCastException` at runtime if `K` is not `Comparable`; either bound `K extends Comparable<K>` or use `LinkedHashMap`. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/MultiListParam.java:119-122` — `comp.get(i)` without checking `comp.size() == ref.size()`; `IndexOutOfBoundsException` when the current list is shorter than the default. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/MultiParam.java:57`, `MultiListParam.java:60`, `ListParam.java:54` — `get()` returns the internal mutable collection; callers can bypass validation entirely. [design] Fix: return unmodifiable view.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/ListParam.java:96` — `defaultValues == null` makes `hasDefaultValue()` always true even if values were set. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/ParamSet.java:33,96` — `rapaio.core.param` imports `rapaio.ml.model.RegressionModel`: the core layer depends on the ML layer. Replace with a `fullName()`-style interface in core. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/ParamSet.java:95-131` — `format(null)` throws NPE (`value.getClass()`), yet `ValueParam` and `ListParam` permit null defaults/values. Reflection picks *any* `name()` method (e.g. `Var.name()`, `Enum.name()`), and `setAccessible(true)` may fail under modules. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/ParamSet.java:74-77` — `copyParameterValues` NPEs when the source set lacks a key; message on line 68 has typo "prameter". [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/ValueParam.java:38-42` — fields are `protected` and `value` is mutable; no subclass in the codebase needs them. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/param/Param.java:54` — default `copyFrom` casts `param.get()` unchecked; `ListParam.copyFrom` casts the private field of a different instance. Both are unsound if two `ParamSet`s register params with the same name but different types. [design]

#### rapaio.core.distributions

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Gamma.java:371-388` — `pdf`, `cdf` and `sampleNext` treat `beta` as a **scale** (`x/beta`, `gds*beta`), but `mean() = alpha/beta`, `var() = alpha/beta²` are the **rate** formulas and `skewness() = 2/sqrt(beta)` should be `2/sqrt(alpha)`. Moments disagree with the density. [bug] Fix: `mean = alpha*beta`, `var = alpha*beta*beta`, `skewness = 2/sqrt(alpha)`, `mode = alpha>=1 ? (alpha-1)*beta : 0`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Gamma.java:116-118` — `pdf(x<0)` returns NaN while `cdf(x<0)` returns 0 and every other distribution returns 0 for out-of-support pdf. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Gamma.java:145-164` — `quantile(p)` with `p > 1` (or NaN) never terminates: `while (cdf(up) <= p) up *= 2` runs until `up = +Inf` and `cdf(Inf) = 1 <= p` forever. Same pattern in `ChiSquare.java:100-101` and `Poisson.java:102-105`. [bug] Fix: validate `0 <= p <= 1` first (as `Uniform`, `DUniform`, `StudentT` do).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/ChiSquare.java:104-116` — bisection stops only on `|p - cdf(mid)| < 1e-14`; for tiny `p` (e.g. 1e-300) or when `cdf` cannot represent `p` exactly the loop can spin forever because `low`/`high` converge but the value criterion is never met. Use an interval-width criterion (as `Gamma` does) or `MathTools.invIncGamma`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/ChiSquare.java:82` — `pdf(0)` evaluates `(df/2-1)*log(0)`: for `df == 2` this is `0 * -Inf = NaN` (should be 0.5); for `df < 2` it should be +Inf. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/ChiSquare.java:57-59` and `StudentT.java:64-66` — constructors reject `df < 1` with a message saying "greater than zero"; chi-square and t are well defined for `0 < df < 1`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/StudentT.java:147-155` — `var()` returns `df/(df-2)*sigma²` for `1 < df < 2`, which is **negative**; variance is +Inf for `1 < df <= 2`. [bug] Fix: `if (df <= 2) return POSITIVE_INFINITY`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/StudentT.java:171`, `ChiSquare.java:156`, `Gamma.java:397` — `entropy()` throws `IllegalArgumentException("Not implemented")`; there is no argument at fault. Use `UnsupportedOperationException`, or return NaN like `Fisher`/`Hypergeometric` do. Three different "not implemented" conventions (throw / NaN / `Poisson.entropy() == 0`) in one interface. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Poisson.java:155-157` — `entropy()` returns `0`, which is simply wrong (Poisson entropy is ~0.5·log(2πeλ) for large λ). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Poisson.java:62` — `getLambda()` duplicates the record accessor `lambda()`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Normal.java:128` — `entropy()` returns `log(2πe·var)`; the correct value is `0.5·log(2πe·var)`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Normal.java:56` — no validation that `sd > 0`; `Normal.of(0, -1)` is accepted and `pdf` works with `var = 1` (sign lost) while `cdf`/`quantile` receive the negative sd. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/LogNormal.java:116` — `skewness()` computes `sqrt(sigma² - 1)`; the correct term is `sqrt(exp(sigma²) - 1)`. Returns NaN for sigma < 1. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/LogNormal.java:65-72` — `pdf`/`cdf` for `x <= 0` return NaN (`log(x)`), should return 0. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/LogNormal.java:126` vs `Normal.java:128`, `Binomial.java:221` — `LogNormal.entropy` and `Binomial.entropy` are in bits (`log2`), `Normal`, `Exponential`, `Uniform`, `Poisson`, `DUniform` in nats. Interface Javadoc does not say which. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/LogNormal.java:37` — no `serialVersionUID` though `Distribution extends Serializable` and every sibling declares one; uses `java.lang.Math` while siblings use `StrictMath`; is a class with private ctor while `Uniform`, `Exponential`, `Fisher`, `Poisson`, `Gamma` are records. Mixed class/record style across siblings. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/LogNormal.java:90-92` — overrides only `sampleNext()` (allocating a new `Normal` and a new `Random` per draw) but not `sampleNext(Random)`, so seeded sampling silently falls back to the interface default. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Distribution.java:103-115` — default `sampleNext()` creates `new Random()` on every call; `sample(n)` therefore allocates n `Random` instances and gets poorly separated seeds. Use a shared `ThreadLocalRandom` or require the caller to pass one. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Uniform.java:60-62` — `pdf` returns 1 when `a == b` (degenerate distribution; should be +Inf or the ctor should reject `a >= b`); `cdf(a)` for `a == b` is `0/0 = NaN`. Record ctor has no validation `a < b`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Exponential.java:72-79` — `quantile(p<0)` returns 0 (should throw as `Uniform` does), `quantile(p>1)` returns NaN; no `lambda > 0` validation. [inconsistency]

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/DUniform.java:44-46,54` — int parameters stored as `double` and cast back in `a()`/`b()`; `discrete()` lacks `@Override`; error text "must be interface the range". [nit]

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Fisher.java:62-63` — `pdf` has no `x <= 0` guard: `pow(x, df1/2-1)` is +Inf for `df1 < 2` at 0 and NaN for negative x. No `df > 0` validation; `kurtosis()` returns NaN although a closed form exists for `df2 > 8`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Hypergeometric.java:220-222` — `cdf`: `if (x > n) return 1.0` is wrong; `n` is the number of black balls, not an upper bound of the support (`min(m,k)`). Example m=10,n=2,k=5: `cdf(2.5)` returns 1 but true value is 0. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Hypergeometric.java:249-250` — `maxValue()` returns `m`; support maximum is `min(m, k)`. `quantile` also iterates to `m` and returns `m` for `p == 1`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Hypergeometric.java:255,270,275` — `m * k`, `n * m * k * (n + m - k)` are `int` products evaluated before widening; overflow for moderately large urns (e.g. m=n=k=2000 → 3.2e13). [bug] Fix: cast to double first.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Hypergeometric.java:274-276` — `skewness()` returns `sqrt((N-1)/(m n k (N-k)))`, missing the `(N-2m)(N-2k)/(N-2)` factor of the standard formula. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Hypergeometric.java:110-117` — `pdf(NaN)`: `(int) rint(NaN) == 0` and `abs(0 - NaN) > 1e-30` is false, so `pdf(NaN)` returns `pdf(0)`. `pdf(±Inf)` throws while `cdf(±Inf)` returns 0/1. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Hypergeometric.java:124-210` — `pdf` allocates two `int[m+n+1]` arrays and runs an O(m+n) factor-cancellation loop per uncached call; `exp(logBinomial(...))` via `lnGamma` would be O(1) and is already available in `MathTools`. Also `pdfCache` is mutated lazily from a nominally immutable, `Serializable` object (benign data race, but not documented). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Hypergeometric.java:70-82` — constructor does not reject `k < 0` (then `new double[k+1]` throws `NegativeArraySizeException`). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Bernoulli.java:59` — `name()` prints raw `prob` while every sibling uses `floatFlex`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Distribution.java:181` — `var()` in `Distribution` vs `variance()` in `OnlineStat`/`WeightedOnlineStat` vs `Variance` class: three names for one concept. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/Distribution.java:193-198` — Javadoc does not state that `kurtosis()` is *excess* kurtosis (all implementations return excess). [nit]

#### rapaio.core.distributions.empirical

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/empirical/KDE.java:134-142` — `silvermanBandwidth` implements the normal-reference rule `1.06·sd·n^-1/5`; Silverman's rule of thumb is `0.9·min(sd, IQR/1.34)·n^-1/5`. Name promises a different, more robust estimator. [inconsistency] Also `sd == 0 → sd = 1` is an arbitrary fallback.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/empirical/KDE.java:99-110` — `Arrays.binarySearch` on an array with duplicates returns an arbitrary index of the run; for `KFuncUniform` (non-zero at `|u| == 1`) points equal to `x ± h` are included or excluded at random, and the exact-hit `to` index is excluded from the loop. Use lower/upper-bound searches. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/empirical/KDE.java:91-95` — no validation `bandwidth > 0` or non-empty sample; `pdf` divides by `values.length * bandwidth`. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/empirical/KDE.java:39` — `KDE` does not implement `Distribution` (no `cdf`, `quantile`, `sample`), so it cannot be used where a `Distribution` is expected (e.g. `KSTestOneSample`). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/empirical/KFuncGaussian.java:48-55` — Gaussian kernel is truncated at ±4h without documenting that the estimate then integrates to ~0.99994. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/distributions/empirical/KFunc*.java` — all nine kernels are stateless yet instantiated with `new` on every `KDE.of` call; an enum or shared singletons would be simpler. `toContent` returns `toString()` in each; the interface already provides two of the three `Printable` defaults, the third could be default too. [design]

#### rapaio.core.tests

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ChiSqConditionalIndependence.java:65-67` — `degrees`, `statistic`, `pValue` are **`static`** fields; `statistic += ...` accumulates across instances, so the second test constructed in a JVM returns a wrong statistic and p-value, and concurrent use is unsafe. [bug] Fix: make them instance `final` fields.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ChiSqConditionalIndependence.java:75-87` — iterates over `z.levels()`, which in rapaio nominal vars includes the missing label `"?"` at index 0; the "?" stratum yields an empty table and the df count `z.levels().size()` is one too many. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ChiSqConditionalIndependence.java:128-130` — printed null hypothesis says `P(x,y|z) = P(x|z) + P(y|z)`; independence is a product. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ChiSqIndependence.java:114` — Yates correction `|O-E| - 0.5` is not clamped at 0; when `|O-E| < 0.5` the squared term grows instead of shrinking. R uses `min(0.5, |O-E|)`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ChiSqIndependence.java:112-114` — zero row/column totals give `exp = 0` and a NaN/Inf statistic without a message. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ChiSqGoodnessOfFit.java:117-123` — the second `if (abs(e) < 1e-50 && ...) continue;` is unreachable (the previous `if` breaks on the same condition). Message "Different degrees of freedom!" on line 101 describes a length mismatch; "goodness of predict" on line 158 should be "goodness of fit". No check that `p` sums to 1. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ChiSqGoodnessOfFit.java:130` `df()` vs `ChiSqIndependence.java:121` `getDegrees()` vs `ChiSqConditionalIndependence.java:94` `double getDegrees()` vs `TTestOneSample.getDegrees()` int vs `TTestTwoPaired.getDegrees()` double — same concept, four names/types. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/KSTestTwoSamples.java:65-66` — off-by-one: `fn1 = i1++ / n1` uses the 0-based index *before* increment, so the ECDF lags by `1/n`. Example `v1={1}`, `v2={2}` gives `D = 0` instead of 1. (Numerical Recipes used 1-based `j1`.) [bug] Fix: `fn1 = ++i1 / n1`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/KSTestOneSample.java:65-78` and `KSTestTwoSamples.java:52-53` — missing values are not filtered (every other test in the package drops them); a single NaN makes `cdf(NaN)` NaN and `D = max(D, NaN) = NaN`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/KSTestOneSample.java:84-105` and `KSTestTwoSamples.java:74-95` — `probks` is duplicated verbatim. Move to `MathTools`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/KSTestOneSample.java:60`, `KSTestTwoSamples.java:47`, `ADTestGoodness.java:47`, `ChiSq*.from` vs `TTest*.test`/`ZTest*.test` — factory method is `from` in half the tests and `test` in the other half. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ADTestGoodness.java:87-93` — when `mu` is given but `sigma` is not, `sigmaHat = sqrt(Σ(x-mu)²)` is missing the division by `n`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ADTestGoodness.java:112-113` — the same fully-specified (case 0) Marsaglia p-value function is applied to `a2star` (parameters estimated, case 3). Case-3 p-values need Stephens' adjusted critical values; as written `pValueStar` is systematically too large. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ADTestGoodness.java:47-53` — `NaN` is used as the "not provided" sentinel for `mu`/`sigma` via the public `from(Var, double, double)`; a caller who passes a genuinely computed NaN silently switches to estimation. Use overloads or `OptionalDouble`. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ADTestGoodness.java:106-107` — `log(phi)`/`log(1-phi)` with `phi` exactly 0 or 1 (extreme outliers) yield `-Inf` and `a2 = +Inf`; no clamping. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/TTestOneSample.java:157-165` — guard is `sampleSize < 1`, but `sampleSize == 1` gives `sd = NaN` and `StudentT.of(0)` **throws** `IllegalArgumentException`. Same in `TTestTwoPaired.java:100` (`df = 0`) and `TTestTwoSamples.java:206` (`df` may be 0 or `< 1` for Welch). [bug] Fix: guard on `< 2` / `df < 1`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/TTestOneSample.java:188-203` computes one-sided CIs for one-sided alternatives (as R does), but `TTestTwoSamples.java:346-347`, `TTestTwoPaired.java:126-127`, `ZTestOneSample.java:191-192`, `ZTestTwoSamples.java:257-258`, `ZTestTwoPaired.java:131-132` always return the two-sided interval regardless of `alt`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/TTestOneSample.java:186` — local `var t` shadows the field `t`; `this.t` and `t` are both used in the same block. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/TTestTwoPaired.java:176` — summary header says "Two Paired z-test" for a t-test; `TTestTwoSamples.java:354`, `ZTestOneSample.java:199`, `ZTestTwoSamples.java:265` print the stale name `HTTools.*`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/TTestTwoSamples.java:211-215` — uses `-1` as sentinel sample size / df in the degenerate case while `TTestOneSample` keeps the real size and NaN statistics. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/TTestTwoPaired.java:92` and `ZTestTwoPaired.java:114` — paired tests silently pair up to `min(x.size(), y.size())` rows; mismatched lengths are a caller error and should throw. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/ZTestTwoPaired.java:101-110` — degenerate check tests `xComplete`/`yComplete` separately; the pairwise-complete set can still be empty, producing `sv = sd/0 = Inf`, `zScore = NaN` without going through the guarded branch. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/tests/HTest.java:36-38` — `Alternative.pCondition()` strings hard-code `z` ("P > |z|") and are printed by t-tests too. Naming `TWO_TAILS`/`GREATER_THAN`/`LESS_THAN` differs from R's `two.sided`/`greater`/`less`, which the docs reference. [nit]
- All `*Test` classes — mixed accessor styles: `getMu()`, `getSl()`, `getT()` next to `pValue()`, `ciLow()`, `d()`. Statistic fields are non-final in `TTestOneSample`/`ZTest*`/`KSTest*` but final in `TTestTwoPaired`/`ZTestTwoPaired`. `sl` for significance level is opaque; `alpha` is the standard name. [inconsistency]
- `KSTestOneSample`/`KSTestTwoSamples`/`ChiSq*`/`ADTestGoodness` return `NaN` from `ciLow()/ciHigh()` because the `HTest` interface forces CI methods on tests that have no CI. Split `HTest` into `HTest` and `HTestWithCI`. [design]

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/SamplingTools.java:342-345` — Vose alias table clean-up `while (smallPos > 0) prob[dq[smallPos-1]] = 1` skips `dq[smallPos]` (entries are `dq[0..smallPos]`), leaving one bucket with `prob` slightly below 1 and `alias == 0`, a tiny bias toward index 0. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/SamplingTools.java:186-219` — `sampleWeightedWOR(random, 0, freq)` with non-empty `freq` reaches `k[heap[1]]` with `heap[1] == -1` → `ArrayIndexOutOfBounds`. Zero weights give `pow(u, Inf) = 0` keys and `log(0)` in `xw`, relying on IEEE corner cases. [bug]

[inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/core/SamplingTools.java:367` — `randomSampleSlices` truncates each slice with `(int)(freq*n)` and dumps all rounding remainder into the last slice. [nit]

#### Test coverage (tst/rapaio/core)

- No tests at all for `rapaio.core.param` (`ValueParam`, `ListParam`, `MultiParam`, `MultiListParam`, `ParamSet`) — the three validation/`hasDefaultValue` bugs above would have been caught. [test]
- No tests for `LogNormal`, `Exponential`, `Grid2D`, `ChiSqGoodnessOfFit` beyond one case, `WeightedOnlineStat.update(WeightedOnlineStat)`, `OnlineStat.update(OnlineStat)` sum/max after merge. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/core/distributions/GammaTest.java:102-105` — asserts `Gamma.of(3,2).mean() == 1.5` (rate) while lines 61-65 and `gamma.csv` (generated with `dgamma(shape, scale)`) test the density as scale; the test enshrines the moment/density mismatch and the wrong `skewness = 2/sqrt(beta)`. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/core/distributions/HypergeometricTest.java:66` — asserts `skewness == 0.018…` for `(m=20, n=20, k=30)`; the true value is 0 (N = 2m). Test enshrines the wrong formula. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/core/stat/KurtosisTest.java:48` — asserts the `bigG2` produced by the wrong denominator `(n-2)*n-3`; needs a reference value from R `e1071::kurtosis(type=2)`. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/core/tests/KSTestTest.java:55-58` — two-sample D is checked with tolerance `1e-4` against 0.150278 on a large sample, which masks the `1/n` off-by-one; add a tiny hand-computable case (`{1}` vs `{2}` → D = 1). [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/core/SamplingToolsTest.java` — no test of the stratified split proportions or of `trainTestSplit` default weights; no test that the input `freq` array is left untouched. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/core/tests/ChiSqTest.java:123` — conditional-independence test runs once per JVM so the `static` accumulator bug is invisible; run two instances in one test. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/core/tests/ADTestGoodnessTest.java` — one test; the `mu`-given/`sigma`-estimated branch (missing `/n`) is never exercised. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/core/stat/QuantilesTest.java` — 2 tests; no all-missing / single-value / `p` outside [0,1] cases. [test]
- Distribution tests never call `quantile(1.0)`/`quantile(1.5)` on `Gamma`, `ChiSquare`, `Poisson` (would hang), `pdf(0)` on `ChiSquare(2)`, `var()` on `StudentT(1.5)`, `cdf(-1)` on `Binomial`, `entropy()` on `Normal`/`Poisson`. [test]

#### Summary (top 5)

1. **Gamma moments disagree with its density** (`Gamma.java:371-388`): pdf/cdf/sampler use `beta` as scale, `mean/var` use it as rate, `skewness = 2/sqrt(beta)` is wrong in either reading; tests enshrine it.
2. **Stratified train/test split ignores the requested proportions** (`SamplingTools.java:387-415`) and `trainTestSplit` default weights are row indices (`:445`); `normalize()` mutates caller arrays.
3. **`ChiSqConditionalIndependence` keeps its statistic in `static` fields** (`:65-67`) — every instance after the first is wrong; not thread-safe.
4. **A cluster of wrong closed-form results**: `Kurtosis.bigG2` denominator (`Kurtosis.java:74`), `Normal.entropy` missing ½ (`Normal.java:128`), `LogNormal.skewness` (`:116`), `StudentT.var` negative for 1<df<2 (`:147`), `Hypergeometric.cdf/skewness/maxValue/int overflow`, `KSTestTwoSamples` ECDF off by 1/n (`:65`), `ADTestGoodness.sigmaHat` missing `/n` (`:89-93`), `OnlineStat.max` initialised to 0.
5. **Unbounded/hanging or throwing edge cases at public boundaries**: `Gamma`/`ChiSquare`/`Poisson.quantile(p>1)` loop forever; `TTest*` with one observation throw from `StudentT.of(0)`; `Quantiles` returns wrong-length/zero arrays for 0–1 complete values; `HistogramTable` with constant data throws; `DensityTable` is not actually serializable.

### 2.2 data (Var, Frame, mapping, index, group, transform, ...)

# Review: rapaio-lib `rapaio.data` (src + tst)

Root: `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data`. Severity tags: [bug] [inconsistency] [design] [test] [nit].

#### Var.java / AbstractVar.java (base contract)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/Var.java:561` `deepEquals(Var,double)`: for INSTANT, when both rows are missing the loop still runs `getInstant(i).equals(...)` on a `null` -> NPE. [bug] Fix: `if (isMissing(i)) continue;` after the missing-equality check.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/Var.java:576` `deepEquals`: no `FLOAT` case, so `VarFloat` is compared by `getLabel` string equality and ignores `tol`. [bug] Add `case FLOAT, DOUBLE`.

#### Missing-value contract across Var types (cross-cutting)

| type | storage for missing | getDouble(missing) | getInt(missing) | getLong(missing) | setDouble(NaN) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| VarDouble | NaN | NaN | `0` (rint(NaN)) | `0` | missing |
| VarInt | Integer.MAX_VALUE | NaN | MAX_VALUE | `Integer.MAX_VALUE` (not VarLong.MISSING) | missing |
| VarLong | Long.MAX_VALUE | `9.22e18` | `-1` (truncation) | MAX_VALUE | stores `0` |
| VarBinary | bit | NaN | Integer.MAX_VALUE | Long.MAX_VALUE | missing (any non 0/1) |
| VarNominal | index -1 | `-1.0` | `-1` | throws | level 0 (!) |
| VarInstant | null | NPE | NPE | Long.MAX_VALUE | epoch 0 |
| VarString | "?" | throws | throws | throws | throws |

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/VarDouble.java:496,537` `getInt/getLong` on NaN return 0 rather than `VarInt.MISSING_VALUE`/`VarLong.MISSING_VALUE`; `VarInt.getDouble` does the reverse mapping correctly. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/VarLong.java:386,401,416` `getInt/getFloat/getDouble` return the sentinel `Long.MAX_VALUE` coerced (`-1`, `9.2e18`) instead of missing. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/VarLong.java:421,406` `setDouble/setFloat(NaN)` -> `(long) Math.rint(NaN) == 0`, silently stores 0. [bug] Check `Double.isNaN` like VarInt does.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/VarNominal.java:317,322` `getDouble` returns raw index (-1 for missing, not NaN); `setDouble(NaN)` -> `(int) rint(NaN) == 0` -> assigns level 0, corrupting data. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/VarInstant.java:158-191` `getFloat/getDouble/getInt` dereference `data[row]` -> NPE on missing; `setDouble/setFloat(NaN)` -> epoch 0. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/VarInt.java:401` `getLong` on missing returns `Integer.MAX_VALUE` so `VarLong.copy(VarInt)` (`VarLong.java:146`) loses missingness. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/VarString.java:96-100,254` constructor/`addRows` fill with `null`, but `isMissing` tests `"?".equals(v)`; a fresh `VarString.empty(n)` reports all rows as *not* missing and `getLabel` returns `null`. [bug] Either fill with `MISSING_VALUE` or treat `null` as missing.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/data/VarString.java:46` `"?"` as missing sentinel means a legitimate string "?" cannot be stored. [design]

#### Exception contract for unsupported operations

- `VarDouble.levels()` throws `RuntimeException` (`VarDouble.java:527`), `VarInt` throws `IllegalStateException` (`VarInt.java:392`), `VarLong` throws `IllegalArgumentException` (`VarLong.java:447`), `VarBinary.setLevels` throws `IllegalArgumentException` (`VarBinary.java:385`), `VarNominal/VarInstant/VarString` throw `OperationNotAvailableException`. [inconsistency] Use `OperationNotAvailableException` everywhere.
- `VarBinary.levels()` (`VarBinary.java:380`) returns `["true","false"]` — index 0 is "true" while `getInt` returns 1 for true; also inconsistent with the "levels = nominal only" contract. [bug]
