#### rule (OneRule, HolteBinning, ZeroRule, ManualClassifier)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/onerule/HolteBinning.java:83` [bug] inside the sorted-position loop the target label is read with `df.getLabel(j, ...)` (position in the sorted `rows` array) instead of `df.getLabel(rows[j], ...)`, and the weight uses `rows[i]` instead of `rows[j]`. Bucket histograms are built from the wrong rows unless the data happens to be pre-sorted. `HolteBinningTest` only uses already-sorted inputs, hiding this. Fix: `df.getLabel(rows[j], target)`, `weights.getDouble(rows[j])`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/onerule/HolteBinning.java:132-134` [bug] the missing-value bucket loop declares `j` but reads `rows[i]` (i == len, the first missing row) every iteration, so the histogram counts the first missing row's label `n_missing` times. Should be `rows[j]`. It also switches to `df.getInt`+`emptyByLabels(true, ...)` index arithmetic whereas the rest of the file uses labels.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/OneRule.java:143` [bug] density loop starts at `j = 1`, but `firstTargetLevels()` does not contain `?` and `ClassifierResult` allocates one density column per level (ClassifierResult.java:94), so the density of the first target level is never written (always 0). `NaiveBayes.corePredict:184` correctly starts at 0. No test in `OneRuleTest` asserts densities.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/OneRule.java:104,189-203` [bug] BINARY inputs are routed to `buildNominal`, which indexes buckets by `getInt()+1` while labeling them from `levels()` = `["true","false"]` (VarBinary.java:380): value 0 → bucket 1 → label "true". At predict time `getLabel` yields the real formatted label, so rules are matched to the wrong/unknown bucket. A missing binary returns `VarInt.MISSING_VALUE = Integer.MAX_VALUE`, `+1` overflows → `ArrayIndexOutOfBounds`. Route BINARY through the numeric path or use label-based indexing.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/OneRule.java:182` [bug] fallback returns class `"?"` with an all-zero density; `corePredict:142` then calls `normalize()` on a zero vector → NaN densities, and `"?"` is written as the predicted class. Reached for: unseen nominal labels, numeric missing with `MissingHandler.CATEGORY` when training had no missing values, and values falling in no interval (should be impossible but `NaN` comparisons make it possible). Fall back to `missingDensity`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/onerule/HolteBinning.java:96-106` [design] Holte's 1R merges *adjacent* intervals that end up with the same majority class after the minCount constraint; here a closed bucket is never merged with the next one even when both predict the same class, producing redundant rules and a slightly different accuracy. Document or add a final merge pass.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/onerule/HolteBinning.java:82` [nit] groups equal values with `==` on doubles; fine for sorted data but `-0.0 == 0.0` differ from the comparator ordering. Use `Double.compare(...) == 0` for consistency with the sort.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/onerule/HolteBinning.java:111` [nit] `set.getRules().size() > 0` → `!isEmpty()`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/OneRule.java:277` [design] `Binning.compute(testVarName, OneRule parent, df, weights)` leaks the whole model into the strategy just to fetch `firstTargetName()`/`firstTargetLevels()`. Pass `targetName` and `targetLevels` explicitly.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/OneRule.java:116` [design] ties between candidate variables resolve to the first column; `RuleSet.getAccuracy()` returns NaN when `total == 0`, and `NaN > x` is false so a NaN ruleset can become and stay `bestRuleSet` if it is the first column.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/OneRule.java:154` [nit] `df.rvar(testVar).isMissing(row)` vs `df.isMissing(row, testVar)` used elsewhere.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/onerule/HolteBinning.java:35` [nit] no `toString()`, so `OneRule.fullName()` prints the object hash for the `binning` parameter; `name()` exists but is unused.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/onerule/RuleSet.java:42-43` [nit] package-private final fields with public getters returning the mutable `rules` list.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/ZeroRule.java:75,98` [bug] BINARY target accepted, but `DensityVector.fromLevelWeights` keys binary as `"0"/"1"` while the density frame columns are `"true"/"false"` → `firstDensity().setDouble(i, "0", 1)` fails (no such column). Same level-naming mismatch as NaiveBayes.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/ZeroRule.java:87` `ManualClassifier.java:83` [inconsistency] parameter named `withDistributions` while the base class and other models use `withDensities`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/ManualClassifier.java:41` [inconsistency] factory is `model()` while every other model uses `newModel()`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/ManualClassifier.java:65-66` [inconsistency] `newInstance()` returns the raw `ClassifierModel<...>` type instead of `ManualClassifier` and hand-copies `rules` instead of `copyParameterValues(this)`; also `majorityLabel` is (correctly) not copied but there is no comment.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/ManualClassifier.java:76-78` [inconsistency] weights are ignored when computing the majority label (ZeroRule/OneRule use weighted counts).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/ManualClassifier.java:89-101` [design] a rule may return a label that is not a target level; `setLabel` silently adds a new level to the prediction var and the density row stays all zeros. Validate against `firstTargetLevels()`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rule/ManualClassifier.java:39` [inconsistency] not `Printable`, no `toString`/`toSummary`, unlike ZeroRule/OneRule/NaiveBayes.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/ml/model/rule/OneRuleTest.java` [test] no assertions on densities, no BINARY input, no unseen nominal label at predict, no unsorted numeric input (would expose HolteBinning `rows[j]` bug), no weighted fit.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/ml/model/rule/onerule/HolteBinningTest.java` [test] all inputs pre-sorted; add a shuffled frame with the same expected rules, and a frame with several missing rows carrying different labels.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/ml/model/rule/ManualClassifierTest.java` [test] 56 lines; no test for a rule returning a non-level label, densities, or `newInstance()` independence.

#### knn (KnnRegression)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:165` [bug] `w.clamp_(1 - eps, eps)` passes `min > max`. `UnaryOpClamp` applies min then max (UnaryOpClamp.java:296-300), so every normalized distance becomes `eps`; every kernel is then evaluated at a constant and weighted kNN silently degenerates to a plain k-average for all kernels. Should be `clamp_(eps, 1 - eps)`. `overfitTest` uses k=1 (one neighbour, weight cancels) so it cannot detect this; a k>1 test comparing RECTANGULAR vs INV would.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:136-146` [bug] `TreeSet<Integer>` ordered only by distance treats equally-distant neighbours as duplicates: `add` silently drops the second tied point, so the top set may hold fewer than `k` entries and `indexes` (:147) is padded with zeros → row 0 is used as a fake neighbour. Break ties by index (`thenComparingInt`) or use a bounded priority queue on a precomputed distance array.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:179,124` [bug] `k+1 > n` is not rejected (`coreFit` only checks `n >= 2`), so with `k >= n` `computeTop` returns zero-padded indexes and `ref = topIndexesEx[k]` is row 0. Validate `k.get() + 1 <= df.rowCount()` in `coreFit`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:161-163` [bug] `wref` (distance to the (k+1)-th neighbour) can be 0 when the query coincides with ≥ k+1 training points → `w.div_(0)` gives NaN/Inf; NaN is not clamped and poisons the prediction. Guard `wref <= 0` (fall back to uniform weights).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:137-138` [design] the comparator recomputes `distance.compute` on every comparison: O(n·k·log k) distance evaluations per query instead of O(n). Precompute a `double[] dist` once per query.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:81` [dead code] `wdistance` parameter is declared, documented and tested (`KnnRegressionTest:30`) but never used; either use it in `computeWeights` (that was clearly the intent) or delete it.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:123` [inconsistency] instance `weights` are accepted and ignored (no weighted average); other regressions (L1/L2/Constant) honour them.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:204` [nit] enum constant `TRIANGLUAR` is misspelled (public API).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:191-247` [design] `Kernel.transform(d, k)` — only GAUSSIAN uses `k`; RECTANGULAR returns 0.5 (fine after normalisation) but INV returns `1/v` unbounded when `eps` is tiny (1e-100 in tests → 1e100 weights, precision loss).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:62` [inconsistency] no `@Serial serialVersionUID`, no `toString`/`toSummary`/`Printable`, unlike every other model in scope.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:93,127` [nit] raw `DArray[]` with `@SuppressWarnings("unchecked")`; a single `DArray<Double>` matrix (n × p) would avoid the generic array and allow vectorised distances.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegression.java:109` [design] inputs are declared `allowMissing=false` but no check at predict time; a NaN feature yields NaN distances that `Double.compare` orders last, silently excluding the row's true neighbours.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/ml/model/knn/KnnRegressionTest.java` [test] only k=1 and parameter copy are tested. Missing: k>1 with distinct kernels giving different predictions, ties in distance, k ≥ n, duplicate query points (wref=0), INT/BINARY targets.

#### meta (CStacking)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:114,132` [inconsistency] fit builds the stacker's frame from density columns `j = 1..max-1` (dropping the first target level, since `firstTargetLevels()` no longer contains `?`), but predict emits `j = 0..max-1`. It works only because the stacker selects by fitted `inputNames`; the intent (skip the `?` column) is stale. Use the same range in both places (0..max) or drop one column consistently and comment why.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:111-113` [design] the meta-learner is trained on the weak learners' in-sample predictions on the same `df`, the textbook stacking overfitting pitfall; use out-of-fold predictions (k-fold) for the level-1 training frame.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:110,119` [design] every weak learner and the stacker get the identical `seed`; identical randomized learners (e.g. 4×CTree with random splits, as in `smokeTest`) become fully correlated. Derive per-learner seeds (`seed + i`) or a `Random(seed)` stream.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:75` [bug] `fullName()` returns `"CStacking{stacker=...;"` — unbalanced brace, learners not listed.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:102` [dead code] `learned = false;` inside `coreFit` — the base class assigns `learned = coreFit(...)` (ClassifierModel.java:267) right after.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:58` [inconsistency] field `stackModel` registered under parameter name `"stacker"`; every other model uses the field name as the parameter name.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:81` [design] capabilities restrict inputs to BINARY/INT/DOUBLE although weak learners such as CTree accept NOMINAL; the capability should be the intersection of the learners' capabilities, or at least include NOMINAL.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:136` [nit] `stack.predict(SolidFrame.byVars(vars))` always computes classes and densities regardless of `withClasses/withDistributions`; parameter named `withDistributions` (vs `withDensities`).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStacking.java:47` [inconsistency] `implements Printable` but overrides none of `toSummary/toContent/toFullContent`, and has no `toString`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/meta` [design] the package contains only stacking; there is no weighted-vote / averaging meta classifier, which would be the simpler and more common combiner.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/ml/model/meta/CStackingTest.java` [test] `smokeTest` only checks accuracy > 0.9 on iris; no test that `newInstance()` produces independent weak learners, no multiclass density-shape assertion (would document the j=1 vs j=0 asymmetry), no test with weights.

#### simple (ConstantRegression, L1Regression, L2Regression, RandomValueRegression)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/L1Regression.java:92` `L2Regression.java:78` [bug] instance `weights` are accepted by `coreFit` but the fitted median / mean are unweighted (`Quantiles.of`, `Mean.of`). A weighted L2 baseline should be the weighted mean and L1 the weighted median; otherwise document that weights are ignored (RegressionModel's contract implies they are honoured).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/L1Regression.java:67-68` `L2Regression.java:58-59` [bug] `newInstance()` returns `new X()` without `copyParameterValues(this)`, so the inherited `seed`/`runs` parameters (RegressionModel.java:77,92) are silently dropped, unlike `ConstantRegression`/`RandomValueRegression`. Same in `ZeroRule.newInstance()`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/ConstantRegression.java:53` [bug] `constant` validator is `x -> true`, so `constant.set(null)` is accepted and `corePredict:81` NPEs on auto-unboxing. Use `Objects::nonNull` like `RandomValueRegression.distribution`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/ConstantRegression.java:46,57` `L1Regression.java:57` `RandomValueRegression.java:52,56` [inconsistency] factories are `with(c)`, `newL1()`, `newRVR()`/`from(dist)` while the rest of the library uses `newModel()`. `ConstantRegression` also lacks the private constructor the siblings have.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/ConstantRegression.java:69,80` [inconsistency] capabilities allow exactly one target but `corePredict` loops over `targetNames` as if many; L1/L2 allow up to 1_000_000 targets. Pick one contract for all baselines.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/L1Regression.java:130-172` `L2Regression.java:121-163` [duplicated code] `toContent` and `toFullContent` are byte-identical in each class except for the final `toContent`/`toFullContent` call, and the two classes duplicate each other; L2 even keeps the variable name `median` (:132,:154) for means. Factor a `fittedValuesFrame()` helper into a shared abstract base.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/L2Regression.java:43,170-173` [inconsistency] implements `ParametricEquals` with `equalOnParams` always `true`, while `L1Regression` (parameter-identical) does not implement it.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/L1Regression.java:76` `L2Regression.java:96` [design] `getMedians()/getMeans()` return the internal mutable array.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/RandomValueRegression.java:91` [ok/design] correctly uses `getRandom()` (seeded) and passes it to `sampleNext(random)`; note that `seed == 0` (the default) means a fresh unseeded `Random` per predict (RegressionModel.java:344), so results are non-reproducible unless the user sets a seed — worth documenting on the class.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/RandomValueRegression.java:63` [design] the default `Uniform.of(0,1)` is a shared static-like instance reused across all models created via `newInstance()`; fine while `Distribution` is stateless, fragile otherwise.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/simple/L1Regression.java:115-123` `L2Regression.java:106-114` [nit] `toString` prints at most 5 targets; separator logic tests `i < targetNames.length - 1` producing a trailing `,...` — fine, but duplicated in both classes.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/ml/model/simple/L1RegressionResultTest.java`, `L2RegressionResultTest.java` [test] 285 lines each are almost entirely printed-output snapshots; no test with non-uniform weights (would document the ignored-weights behaviour), none with missing target values, none for `newInstance()` keeping `seed`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/ml/model/simple/ConstantRegressionResultTest.java` [test] no test for `constant.set(null)` or for `with(c)` vs `newInstance()` equality.

#### Summary (top 5)

1. `BernoulliEstimator.java:123` normalizes on the wrong axis and stores P(class | x) instead of the likelihood P(x | class); the test file cements the wrong numbers. Silent bias in every NaiveBayes with binary features.
2. `MultinomialEstimator.java:170` `fit()` always returns `false`, making the estimator unusable inside `NaiveBayes` (throws "cannot be fitted"). Never tested through the model.
3. `KnnRegression.java:165` `clamp_(1-eps, eps)` has min/max swapped, so all kernel weights collapse to a constant and weighted kNN silently equals uniform kNN; plus tie-dropping `TreeSet` and no `k+1 <= n` check (:136-147,:179).
4. `HolteBinning.java:83,133` index the frame by sorted position (`j`, `rows[i]`) instead of `rows[j]`, so OneRule numeric buckets are built from the wrong rows on any unsorted input; tests only use sorted data. `OneRule.java:143` also never writes the first level's density.
5. Cross-cutting: BINARY targets are accepted by NaiveBayes/ZeroRule but level naming differs (`"true"/"false"` vs `"0"/"1"`) → NaN/exception; `NaiveBayes.newInstance()` shares mutable `Estimator`/`Prior` objects; estimators disagree on the unknown-level return value (0 / eps / NaN) and none handle missing inputs or instance weights despite the capabilities saying so.

### 2.5 ml.model linear, svm, rvm, km and ml.analysis

# Review: rapaio.ml.model.{linear,svm,rvm,km}, ml.analysis, ml.eval, ml.loss, ml.common

Root: `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib`. Analysis only, no code changed.

#### ml.model.linear (OLS / Weighted / Ridge / result)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/LinearRegressionResult.java:104-105` (`buildComplete`) — `degrees = n - p` can be 0 or negative; `rss/degrees` yields ±Inf/NaN and `sqrt(m_beta_hat*var)` propagates. Only the p-value is guarded (`degrees < 1`). [bug] Guard `degrees <= 0` once and set std.err/t/p to NaN.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/LinearRegressionResult.java:115-116` — standard errors use the plain OLS formula `(X'X)^-1 * sigma^2` for every `BaseLinearRegressionModel`, including `RidgeRegressionModel` (should be `(X'X+λI)^-1 X'X (X'X+λI)^-1`) and `WeightedLinearRegression` (should use `(X'WX)^-1`). The printed summary is therefore wrong for two of the three models. [bug] Move std-error computation to a model hook, or only compute it for plain OLS.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/LinearRegressionResult.java:169-171` — `fdegree1 = inputs - 1` assumes an intercept column is present; with `intercept=false` the F-test dof is off by one, and with intercept-only models it is 0 → division by zero. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/LinearRegressionResult.java:167` — adjusted R² formula assumes intercept present (R uses a different dof when there is none). [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/LinearRegressionResult.java:110-114` — loop rebinds `features` on every `Intercept` name encountered; a single `if (needsIntercept)` outside the loop is clearer, and `.copy()` of the whole frame is unnecessary. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/LinearRegressionResult.java:148-160` vs `impl/BaseLinearRegressionModel.java:135-150` — the coefficient table is rendered twice (model `toSummary` and result `toSummary` without residuals), with different formatters (`floatMedium` vs `floatFlex`). [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/LinearRegressionModel.java:47` and `WeightedLinearRegression.java:47` — identical `serialVersionUID = 8595413796946622895L` (copy/paste). Harmless but signals duplication. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/LinearRegressionModel.java:70` — `coreFit` silently ignores `weights`; `WeightedLinearRegression` is a separate class whose only difference is 2 lines. A single model with weights (weights default to 1) would remove the duplicate class. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/WeightedLinearRegression.java:70` — no check that weights are non-negative; `sqrt` of a negative weight yields NaN silently. [bug] Validate at boundary.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/RidgeRegressionModel.java:59` — `lambda` validator `Double::isFinite` accepts negative λ; should require `>= 0`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/RidgeRegressionModel.java:151-165` — when `intercept=false`, `centering`/`scaling` are computed in `prepareFit` but never applied; the parameters are silently ignored. Either apply scaling without centering or document/validate. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/RidgeRegressionModel.java:138-140` — `scaling=SD` on a constant column gives `sd=0` → division by zero → NaN coefficients with no error. [bug] Guard `sd==0 → 1`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/RidgeRegressionModel.java:168-171` — solves normal equations `(X'X+λI)β = X'Y` with QR of the Gram matrix; squares the condition number. Cholesky (SPD by construction when λ>0) or an augmented-QR would be cheaper and more stable. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/RidgeRegressionModel.java:118` — `weights` ignored entirely (no weighted ridge), unlike sibling model. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/RidgeRegressionModel.java:114` — `FitSetup.valueOf(fitSetup.df, fitSetup.w, fitSetup.targetVars)` re-wraps an identical object. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/RidgeRegressionModel.java:180-195` — the intercept back-transformation loop with `offset` arithmetic is correct but hard to follow; a `Map<name, scaledIndex>` would remove the offset logic. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/Scaling.java:40` — `SD` uses the biased (population) sd while most of the library/tests use sample sd; document or use `sdValue()`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/impl/BaseLinearRegressionModel.java:57-63` — `firstCoefficients()`/`getCoefficients()` return views into `beta` (`selsq`); callers can mutate model state. [design] Return copies or document.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/impl/BaseLinearRegressionModel.java:87-98` — prediction is a triple scalar loop with `df.getDouble(j, inputName)` (name lookup per cell); use `X.mm(beta)` like fit does. [design/perf]
- Tests: `tst/rapaio/ml/model/linear/RidgeRegressionResultTest.java` has no case for `intercept=false`, `lambda=0` equivalence with OLS is covered but constant-column / negative-λ is not. `CenteringTest`/`ScalingTest` only check enum values. [test]

#### ml.model.linear.BinaryLogistic (+ binarylogistic solvers)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/binarylogistic/BinaryLogisticNewton.java:152` — gradient `ng = X'(y-p)` omits the `-λw` penalty term while the Hessian adds `λI`. For `lambda>0` Newton converges to the wrong (unpenalised-gradient) point. IRLS (`BinaryLogisticIRLS.java:147-148`) is correct because the penalty cancels in the working-response form. [bug] Use `ng = X'(y-p) - λw`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/binarylogistic/BinaryLogisticNewton.java:177` — line search evaluates `negativeLogLikelihood(..., wc, ...)` with the *old* `wc` instead of `wcnew`; the penalty term is computed for the wrong vector. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/binarylogistic/BinaryLogisticNewton.java:165-185` — "line search" shrinks the step while the loss keeps decreasing starting from the full step; that is not backtracking (Armijo) and can only shorten steps. If the full step already overshoots badly it still returns it. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/binarylogistic/BinaryLogisticIRLS.java:128` and `BinaryLogisticNewton.java:133` — penalty in the loss is `λ‖w‖₂/2` (norm, not squared norm) while the Hessian uses `λI` (derivative of `λ‖w‖²/2`). Loss and update disagree; convergence test uses the inconsistent loss. [bug] Use `w.inner(w)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/binarylogistic/BinaryLogisticIRLS.java:140-144` and Newton `:145-149` — L2 penalty is added to *every* diagonal entry, including the intercept column. Intercept should not be penalised. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:272` — `Initialize.EXPECTED_LOG_VAR` uses `Math.min(1e-12, mean*(1-mean))`; should be `max`. As written it always returns `log(1e-12) ≈ -27.6` for every coefficient. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:91` — `l1penalty` parameter is declared, documented and printed in `fullName()` but never used by either solver. [bug] Remove or throw if `!= 0`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:167,183` — `runs` (a generic "number of runs" model parameter) is reused as `maxIter`; the meaning differs from other models where `runs` means ensemble rounds. [inconsistency] Add an explicit `maxIter` parameter.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:101`, `BinaryLogisticIRLS.java:49` (1e-20), `BinaryLogisticNewton.java:49` (1e-100) — three different default `eps`; 1e-100 relative tolerance is unreachable in double precision, so the standalone Newton class never reports `converged=true` before `maxIter`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/binarylogistic/BinaryLogisticIRLS.java:112-113` — `nll_delta / nll` divides by zero when the model fits perfectly (`nll==0`); also `it > 1` skips the check on the first real step for no stated reason. [bug/nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/binarylogistic/BinaryLogisticIRLS.java:113-115` — on convergence the freshly computed `wnew`/`nll` are discarded and the *previous* iterate is returned; the iteration that met the tolerance is wasted and `ws`/`nlls` are one entry short. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/binarylogistic/BinaryLogisticIRLS.java:79-84` — `Result.nll()` returns NaN when `nlls.size()==1` although a value exists; `w()` uses `!isEmpty()`. [nit]
- `BinaryLogisticIRLS.java` vs `BinaryLogisticNewton.java` — `Result` record, `negativeLogLikelihood`, Hessian construction and the fit loop are copy-pasted; only `iterate` differs. Extract a shared base. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:256-258` — density columns `0`/`1` are hard-coded as positive/negative. For a nominal target the density frame columns follow the target level order, so when `nominalLevel` selects a level that is not index 0, column names and probabilities are swapped. Test at `tst/.../BinaryLogisticTest.java:297-298` only passes because the default positive label is level 0. [bug] Look up the column index by `positiveLabel`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:198-201` — for BINARY targets `positiveLabel="1"`/`negativeLabel="0"` while `VarBinary.levels()` is `["true","false"]`; and `target.darray_()` is not checked for missing values (NOMINAL branch throws). [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:229-231` — input matrix is built from `df.varStream()` minus the target, relying on frame column order matching `inputNames` order used in `corePredict:246-247`. Use `inputNames` explicitly. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:225` — intercept column named `"Intercept"` literal instead of `AddIntercept.INTERCEPT`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:322-325` — summary table writes rows starting at `i=0`, overwriting the header row and leaving the last row empty (should be `i+1`). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:238` — "not trained" throws `IllegalArgumentException`; `IllegalStateException` fits. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/linear/BinaryLogistic.java:68` — `intercept` is a `Double` scale factor (0 disables); other linear models use a `Boolean intercept`. [inconsistency]
- Tests: no test for `l2penalty>0` with NEWTON vs IRLS agreement (would catch the gradient bug), none for `EXPECTED_LOG_VAR`, none for `nominalLevel` set to a non-first level. [test]

#### ml.model.svm (SvmClassifier / SvmRegression / OneClassSvm wrappers)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/*.java` (all files) — the package is a port of LIBSVM (Chang & Lin; BSD-3 licensed: `svm_train`, `svm_group_classes`, `sigmoid_train`, `multiclass_probability`, `Cache`, `SolverC/SolverNU` follow the C++ source almost line for line) but every file carries only the rapaio Apache header. There is no LIBSVM copyright notice, no reference to the original or to the changes made. BSD-3 requires retaining the copyright notice. [bug/legal] Fix: add the LIBSVM copyright + license text and a "derived from LIBSVM x.y, changes: ..." note in package-info or each file.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/ProblemInfo.java:163-166` `computeParameters` — when exactly two class weights are given, `weightLabel` is overwritten with `{1, -1}`. Labels in this port are level indices `0..k-1` (see `y[i] = target.getInt(i)`), so the weights never match any class and are silently ignored by `svm_train` (LIBSVM only warns). [bug] Fix: delete the block; it is a leftover from LIBSVM's ±1 convention.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/ProblemInfo.java:151-161` — `nrWeight = weighting.size()` but entries whose key is not a known level are skipped, leaving trailing `(label=0, weight=0.0)` pairs; a weight of 0 for label 0 sets `C=0` for the first class. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/ProblemInfo.java:53,61-82` — `y[i] = target.getInt(i)` is the index in the variable's own dictionary, but when the `levels` parameter is set, `levels`/`index` use the user order; `problemInfo.levels().get((int) score)` in `SvmClassifier.corePredict` then maps predictions to the wrong label. Rows whose label is not in `levels` are still trained on. [bug] Fix: `y[i] = index.get(label)` and drop/throw on unknown labels.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/ProblemInfo.java:193-208` `checkValidProblem` — body is entirely commented out (NU-SVC feasibility check); always returns `true`, so an infeasible `nu` fails deep inside the solver instead of at the boundary. [dead code/design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/ProblemInfo.java:44-134` — three `from(...)` factories duplicate the x-splitting and y-conversion loops; also `ProblemInfo` (a record) holds `Random`, all training rows (`xs`) and mutable maps, and is stored in the model, so a serialized `SvmClassifier` carries the entire training matrix, not only the support vectors. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/SvcKernelMatrix.java:38`, `SvrKernelMatrix.java:40`, `OneClassKernelMatrix.java:31` — `new Cache(len, cacheSize * (1 << 20))`: `Cache.size` counts *elements* (see `size -= more` where `more` is a number of doubles) but the argument is bytes; LIBSVM divides by `sizeof(Qfloat)`. The default "100 MB" cache therefore holds 100M doubles = 800 MB. [bug] Fix: divide by 8 (`Double.BYTES`).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/Cache.java:105` — `h.data.pad(0, more, 1)` allocates a new array and copies on every growth; LIBSVM reallocs in place. Fine functionally, but each call also boxes through `Reference<DArray<Double>>`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/Svm.java` — good: no static mutable state, RNG is `prob.random` seeded from the model's `seed` (`ProblemInfo.java:86`), so training is reproducible and `svm_train` is safe to call concurrently on different problems. `svm_cross_validation` (line 848) is public but unused by any model and untested. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/SvmClassifier.java:165-173` — non-probability branch writes `values[j]` for `j < k` into the density frame, but `values` has `k(k-1)/2` entries; for `k = 2` this reads `values[1]` and throws `ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException` when `withDistributions=true` (k=3 happens to fit, which is why the iris tests pass). The block is immediately overwritten by the `dist` loop anyway. [bug] Fix: delete lines 169-173.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/SvmClassifier.java:168,184` — class label set twice per row; `k` recomputed inside the row loop. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/SvmClassifier.java:175-188` — "density" for the non-probability case is a max of pairwise decision values (unbounded, can be negative) but is stored in the same `density` frame that other classifiers fill with probabilities; downstream metrics (`LogLoss`, `ROC`) will read them as probabilities. [design] Fix: document, or apply a softmax / vote count.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/SvmClassifier.java:54,64`, `SvmRegression.java:51,61` — param field `type` is exposed under the name `"penalty"`, field `c` under `"cost"`; `BinarySMO` calls the same thing `c`/`"c"`. [inconsistency]

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/SvmClassifier.java:111`, `SvmRegression.java:115`, `OneClassSvm.java:91` — `modelInfo` is computed and stored but never read anywhere. [dead code]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/SvmRegression.java:94-99` — `newInstance` style differs from every other model (`copy.copyParameterValues(this); return copy;` vs. `return new X().copyParameterValues(this)`); return type is the raw base type instead of `SvmRegression`, so fluent chaining after `newInstance()` is lost (same for `SvmClassifier.newInstance`). [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/SvmRegression.java:140` — `LOGGER.finest("i:%d, score:%f".formatted(i, score))` formats a string per prediction row even when logging is off; use a supplier. [nit/perf]

Duplicates `Svm.svm_predict_values`. [bug/design]

#### ml.model.svm.BinarySMO

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java` (class) — the algorithm, variable names (`I0..I4`, `bUp/bLow`, `fCache`, `Keerthi1/2`, `eps_delta`, `takeStep`, `examineExample`) and structure mirror Weka's `SMO.BinarySMO`, which is GPL-licensed. No attribution is present. If it is a port, the licensing (Apache vs GPL) needs a decision; if it was written from the Keerthi et al. paper, cite the paper. [design/legal]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:588` `predictScore` — linear branch uses `df.getDouble(row, i)` with `i` as the *compacted* index; it must be `_sparseIndices[i]`. Whenever `compactVectors` drops a zero weight (line 555), every following feature is paired with the wrong weight. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:233-246` — with `prob=true` and a linear kernel, `_vectors` is never assigned (`compactVectors` only sets it in the non-linear branch) → `NullPointerException` at line 236. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:236-245` — Platt scaling logistic is fitted on the *support vectors only* (`_vectors`), not on the full training set (and without the Platt out-of-fold / prior-corrected targets). Support vectors are by construction the points near the margin, so the calibration is heavily biased. [bug] Fix: fit on all training scores (ideally cross-validated), or reuse `Svm.sigmoid_train`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:603` — `df.mapVars(inputNames).darray()` (full matrix copy) is executed inside the per-row prediction loop: O(n²·p) prediction. [bug/perf] Fix: hoist out of the loop.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:605` — one `SolidFrame` + `logistic.predict` call per row for probability output; batch it. [design/perf]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:89,94` — `c` and `eps` validators are `Double::isFinite`; `c ≤ 0` makes `L == H == 0` for every pair and the loop terminates with a degenerate model, `eps ≤ 0` never converges. [bug] Fix: `> 0`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:150` — capabilities accept `NOMINAL` inputs, but `dfTrain.mapVars(inputNames).darray()` (line 293) turns them into dictionary indices and feeds them to the kernel as numbers. [bug] Fix: remove NOMINAL or one-hot encode.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:167` — `while (runs-- >= 0 ...)` performs `maxRuns + 1` passes. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:99-104` — `"?"` is used as the "not set" sentinel for `firstLabel`/`secondLabel`, colliding with the nominal missing marker; a user cannot express "class ? vs rest". Use `null`/`Optional` or empty string like `BinaryLogistic.nominalLevel`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:270-277` one-vs-all branch — `label2 = "~" + firstLabel` is a synthetic level that does not exist in the target dictionary; `cr.firstDensity().setDouble(i, label2, ...)` at line 609/613 then fails on lookup. [bug] (Also `oneVsAll` field is written but never read.)
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:612-613` — non-probability densities are `-score`/`score` (signed margins) written into the probability frame; see the SvmClassifier note. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:592,315,804` — `kernel.get()` is read at predict time; changing the parameter after fit yields wrong output. Store the fitted kernel. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:52-63` — class Javadoc is a `todo:` list. Fields `_vectors`, `_b`, `eps_delta`, `linear_weights` violate naming conventions used elsewhere. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/BinarySMO.java:707` `State` — package-private top-level class in the same file as a public class; `initialize` declares `throws IllegalArgumentException` (unchecked). [nit]

#### ml.common.kernel.cache

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/cache/KernelCache.java:44-48,102-103` — `SolidCache` is chosen for `n ≤ 10_000` and allocates `n*n` doubles plus `n*n` booleans up-front: at n = 10 000 that is 800 MB + 100 MB before the first kernel evaluation. Threshold should be ~2 000–3 000 or the cache should be bounded (LRU, as `libsvm.Cache`). [bug]

[inconsistency/design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/cache/KernelCache.java:52-58` — API returns/boxes `Double` (nullable sentinel) on the SMO hot path; use `NaN` or a `boolean` flag array to stay primitive. [design/perf]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/cache/KernelCache.java:76` — `transient` on a field of a non-`Serializable` class has no effect. [nit]

- Tests: no unit test exists for `KernelCache` (symmetry, clear, MapCache path). [test]

#### ml.common (kernel, kernel.cache, distance, VarSelector, ClassWeights)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/SigmoidKernel.java:68` — Javadoc says `tanh(α x·y + c)`, code computes `Math.atan(...)`. Different function, different range. [bug] Use `Math.tanh`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/CauchyKernel.java:61-62` — Javadoc `1/(1+‖x−y‖²/σ²)`; code computes `value = ‖x−y‖²/σ` then `1/(1+value²)` = `1/(1+‖x−y‖⁴/σ²)`. [bug] `return 1/(1 + deltaSumSquares(u,v)/(sigma*sigma))`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/LogKernel.java:53`, `PowerKernel.java:52`, `GeneralizedStudentTKernel.java:54` — all raise `deltaSumSquares` (= ‖x−y‖²) to `degree`, giving ‖x−y‖^{2d} instead of the documented ‖x−y‖^d. [bug] Use `Math.pow(Math.sqrt(dss), degree)` or `Math.pow(dss, degree/2)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/SphericalKernel.java:53-57` and `CircularKernel.java:53-58` — (1) the cut-off test is inverted: returns 0 when `dot < sigma` and evaluates the polynomial outside the support (should be 0 when ‖x−y‖ ≥ σ); (2) `dot` is the *squared* distance but is compared to and divided by σ as if it were the distance. Both kernels are wrong everywhere (and Circular takes `sqrt(1-f²)` of negative numbers → NaN). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/WaveKernel.java:67-71` — uses squared distance where the wave kernel `θ/‖x−y‖ · sin(‖x−y‖/θ)` needs the distance. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/ChiSquareKernel.java:43-45` — `Σ(x−y)²/(x+y)` divides by zero when `x_i+y_i==0` (both zero → 0/0 = NaN, although the term should be 0). [bug] Skip zero-sum coordinates.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/AbstractKernel.java:41-43` — `deltaSumSquares` allocates two temporaries (`sub`, `sqr`) per call; kernels are the innermost hot loop of SMO/RVM. Provide a fused loop. [design/perf]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/*.java` — argument order differs between kernels (`deltaSumSquares(v,u)` vs `(u,v)`, `u.inner(v)` vs `v.inner(u)`); harmless for symmetric kernels but inconsistent. Constructors are placed after `@Override` methods in `PolyKernel`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/cache/KernelCache.java:44-48,102-103` — `SolidCache` is chosen for `n ≤ 10_000` and allocates `n*n` doubles + `n*n` booleans: at n=10 000 that is 800 MB + 100 MB. Threshold is far too high; also stores a full square while only the upper triangle is used. [bug] Lower threshold (~2 000) or store `n(n+1)/2` entries and use a `BitSet`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/cache/KernelCache.java:79-86` — `MapCache` does not canonicalise `(row1,row2)` vs `(row2,row1)` while `SolidCache` does, so for large problems the symmetric entry is computed twice. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/cache/KernelCache.java:76` — `transient` on a field of a non-`Serializable` class has no effect. `MapCache` is unbounded (no eviction) and boxes every value. [nit/design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/cache/KernelCache.java` — not thread-safe (plain `HashMap`, arrays); fine as a per-fit object but should be documented since it is a public class. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/distance/MinkowskiDistance.java:39` — `name()` returns `"Euclidean"`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/distance/MinkowskiDistance.java:54,63` — `pow(delta, p)` without `abs`: negative differences give NaN for non-integer `p` and cancel out for odd integer `p`. [bug] Use `pow(abs(delta), p)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/distance/*.java` — no handling of missing values (NaN propagates to the whole distance); no check that `df2` has the same variables as `df1` (`df1.varNames()` is used for both). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/distance/Distance.java:41` — `Distance` is not `Serializable` while `Kernel` is; models holding a `Distance` parameter (KMCluster, KMedoids) cannot be serialized. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/distance/Constant.java:35` — factory takes `int` while the field is `double`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/VarSelector.java:64-74` — `withVarNames` mutates `this.mCount` (side effect on a value-like object) and constructs the copy with `new VarSelector(mCount)`, so the copy's `mVars` becomes a fixed count: `AUTO`/`ALL` mode is lost (`name()` prints `VarSelector[3]`, `ALL` degenerates to a random permutation in `nextVarNames`, and a second `withVarNames` on a different variable set keeps the stale count). [bug] Keep `mVars` in the copy.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/ClassWeights.java:56-62,71` — `instanceCount` returns raw counts (not inverse frequencies, which is what class weighting usually means) and exposes a mutable `HashMap` while the `of(...)` factories return immutable `Map.of`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/ClassWeights.java:35-53` — five overloads up to 5 classes; a varargs or `Map`-taking factory would scale. [design]

#### ml.model.km (KMCluster, KMClusterInit, KMedoids, MWKMeans)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/km/KMCluster.java:293-303` `repairEmptyClusters` — empty clusters are detected as `Unique.ofInt(assignment)` values whose `rowList` is empty. A value only appears in `Unique` if at least one row has it, so `rowList(i)` is never empty, `emptyCentroids` is always empty and the repair loop is dead. The early return at 294 is the only working path; when a cluster is empty, `recomputeCentroids` calls `Mean.of(emptyVar)` → NaN centroid → every later distance is NaN → all rows assigned to cluster 0. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/km/KMClusterInit.java:63-66` `PlusPlus` — sampling weights are `distance.compute(...)` (D), not D² as k-means++ prescribes (for Euclidean use `reduced`). - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/km/KMClusterInit.java:46` `Forgy` / `PlusPlus` — duplicate rows can yield identical centroids; not guarded (unlike the intent of `checkIfEqual`). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/km/KMCluster.java:194` — `capabilities().inputs(..., allowMissing=true, ...)` but `initialDf.darray()` turns missing into NaN and `EuclideanDistance` has no NaN handling → NaN distances, silent garbage clustering. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/km/KMCluster.java:217`, `MWKMeans.java:182` — `if (runningHook != null)` tests the `ValueParam` object (never null) instead of `runningHook.get()`; harmless because the default hook is a no-op, but misleading and it also sets `learned = true` mid-fit. #### ml.eval (metric, split, evaluation, silhouette, rand index)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:85-88,125,140-143` — matrix is indexed by `getInt()` and labelled by `levels(true).get(i+1)`. For `VarBinary`, `levels()` is `["true","false"]` but `getInt()` returns `1` for true, so row/col 0 is labelled "true" while it holds the *false* cases; `tp` therefore counts (false,false). Orientation bug for binary targets; for nominal targets "positive" is silently whatever level comes first. [bug] Map through `getLabel` → index, and let the user name the positive class.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:145-149` — `mcc`, `f1`, `precision`, `recall`, `g` divide by zero (all-negative predictions, empty class) → NaN with no guard; convention is 0 (sklearn `zero_division`). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:137` — `div_(completeCases)` with zero complete cases → NaN table. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:108-117` — requires identical level lists *in the same order*; a prediction var with the same levels permuted is rejected although the matrix could be built by label. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java` — only binary precision/recall/F1; no macro/micro/per-class versions for k>2 although the matrix is multi-class. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:127-133 vs 144` — `p`/`n` are counted skipping only missing *classes*, but the sweep also skips rows with missing *scores*; `auc /= p*n` and `fpr = fp/n` then use denominators that include rows never swept. [bug] Skip the same rows in both loops.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:177` — `auc /= p*n` is NaN when either class is empty; no guard/exception. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:142,151` — two different sentinels (`Double.MIN_VALUE`, then `POSITIVE_INFINITY`) for "no previous score"; `MIN_VALUE` is the smallest positive double, not a "none" value, so a score exactly equal to it (or `+∞` in the second pass) miscounts rows and `pos` overruns/underruns `len`. [nit] Use a boolean flag.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:69-78` — `from(score, actual, predict)` builds the positive class as "prediction was correct", which is not a ROC curve of a class; the Javadoc should say so or the overload should go. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:90-91` — `from(score, actual, int index)` indexes `levels(true)`, so `index=0` is the missing label `"?"` while everywhere else level indices exclude it. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:213-224` — summary picks 11 rows by `rowCount()/10`; for `rowCount()<11` rows repeat, for `rowCount()==0` index `-1`. [nit]

#### ml.model.rvm (RVMRegression)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:660-661` `EvidenceApproximation.initializeAlphaBeta` — `parent.getRandom().nextDouble()` is called once *per element*; `getRandom()` returns `new Random(seed)` on every call (`RegressionModel.java:343`), so with a non-zero seed all initial alphas are the same number, and with seed 0 the run is non-reproducible even though a `random` was already drawn in `coreFit`. [bug] Fix: pass the `Random` from `coreFit` into the algorithm.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:545-557,611-613` `testConvergence` — after `pruneAlphas()` shrinks `alpha`, `oldAlpha` still has the pre-pruning size; the loop compares `alpha[i]` with `oldAlpha[i]` index-by-index on misaligned vectors, so convergence is judged on the wrong pairs (and would throw if `alpha` were larger). [bug] Fix: compare only surviving indices (`keep`) or test on `m`/`beta` change.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:587-591,678-684` — `sigma = t.lu().inv()` with a `catch (IllegalArgumentException)` fallback to QR; relying on an unchecked exception type for singularity detection is fragile, and a full inverse is computed twice per iteration (once in `fit`, again in `pruneAlphas`). Use Cholesky (matrix is SPD) and solve rather than invert. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:595-602` — update equations `α_i = γ_i / m_i²`, `β = (N − Σγ)/‖y − Φm‖²`, `Σ = (βΦᵀΦ + A)⁻¹`, `m = βΣΦᵀy` match Tipping (2001). Good. But `m_i == 0` gives `α_i = Inf` with no guard before the prune test; only `alphaThreshold` (1e9) catches it. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:645` — `throw new RuntimeException("All vectors pruned.")` from inside `fit`; should be a domain exception or a `converged=false` result with an empty model. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:425-445` `corePredict` — the kernel value `features.get(...).kernel.apply(row)` is computed once for the mean and a second time for the quantile variance; quantile path also allocates `phi_m` per row. [design/perf]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:63,1293` — a 1 300-line class holding three full algorithms (`EvidenceApproximation`, `FastTipping`, `FastOnline`), 4 feature-provider records, a run-info class and a `PhiCache` as nested types; the algorithms should be top-level package-private classes (as `binarylogistic` does). [design]

The existing test still passes because the two remaining class means are centred on the 3-class global mean. [bug] Fix: use all levels; also then `k ≤ levels − 1`.

[bug] Fix: compute `vmean`/`vstd` on raw `mx` once, apply them in both `fit` and `transform`.

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/LDA.java:124,128` — a general (non-symmetric) `eig()` is used and imaginary parts are discarded (`evd.real()`); `S_W⁻¹S_B` can have complex parts numerically. Sort at 133-139 boxes to `Integer[]`. [design]

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:59,64` — `eps` and `maxRuns` are declared but never used (leftover from an iterative eigen solver). [dead code]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:115,124` — standardization uses population sd (`std1d(0, 0)`, ddof 0) while eigenvalues are divided by `n − 1` (sample covariance); mixed conventions, so eigenvalues of standardized data do not sum to `p`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:123-125` — symmetric `XᵀX` decomposed with the general `eig()` and `real()`; a symmetric solver (or SVD of `X`, which is more accurate) is the right tool; eigenvector sign is not normalized (e.g. largest-abs component positive), so results differ from run to run / from R & sklearn conventions.
