#### ml.eval (Confusion, ROC, Gini, LogLoss, RMSE, splits)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:139-149` — binary metrics take level index 0 as the positive class. For `VarBinary`, `levels()` is `["true","false"]` (`VarBinary.java:379`) while `getInt` is `1` for true / `0` for false, so `cmFrequency` row/column 0 holds *false* cases and `tp/fp/fn/precision/recall/F1` are computed for the negative class. Needs a "positive label" argument rather than an index convention. [bug] (verify with a VarBinary actual/predict pair where the classes are unbalanced).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:145-149` — `mcc`, `precision`, `recall`, `f1`, `g` divide by counts that can be 0 (an empty predicted or actual class) and yield NaN with no guard/definition (sklearn defines 0 with a warning). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:85-88` — `factors = actual.levels(true)` (with `"?"`) is used for table headers (`factors.get(i+1)`), but `binary` is decided on `actual.levels().size() == 2`; the two conventions are mixed throughout, which is what produces the off-by-one style code (`factors.size() - 1` everywhere). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:108-117` — requires `actual` and `predict` to have *identical* level lists in the same order; a prediction variable built from a model dictionary with a different level order is rejected instead of being reconciled by label. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:63-79` — `tp/tn/...` and case counters are `double`; `completeCases` is printed with `(int) Math.rint`. Use `int`/`long`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:142` — `prev = Double.MIN_VALUE` is used as a "no previous score" sentinel; it is the smallest *positive* double, not a sentinel; a score exactly equal to it (or any score set starting at it) miscounts distinct thresholds. Use `NaN` and `Double.compare`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:160,177` — `fp / n` and `auc /= p * n` divide by zero when all cases are positive or all negative (`tpr` is guarded at 161, `fpr` and `auc` are not). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:69-78` — `ROC.from(score, actual, predict)` defines the positive class as "prediction equals actual", i.e. a ROC of *correctness*, which is not a ROC curve in the usual sense; misleading overload next to `from(score, actual, label)`. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:213-224` `toSummary` — hard-coded 11 deciles; for `data.rowCount() < 11` rows repeat, and `rows[10] = rowCount - 1` is negative for an empty frame. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Gini.java:79-82` — `totalLosses == 0` (all-zero actuals) divides by zero; `n` is computed but `actual.size()` is used. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/LogLoss.java:63` — `result.firstDensity().getDouble(i, actual.getLabel(i))` fails on a missing actual (`"?"` column) and assumes the density frame is a probability (SVM decision values, see svm notes, are not). Also `eps` clamp `[eps, 1-eps]` is right. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/RMSE.java:65-66` — `totalSum`/`totalCount` are declared and never used; `RMSE` keeps `actual`/`prediction` as instance fields (stateful metric object, not thread-safe, and the fields are never read). NaN in either var propagates silently. [dead code/design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/LogLoss.java:65`, `AbstractClassifierMetric` — `compute(...)` stores the score in the metric and returns `this`, while regression metrics return a fresh `RegressionScore`. Two different contracts for the same concept. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/split/StratifiedKFold.java:63` — `rows.get(df.getInt(i, strataName))` throws `IndexOutOfBounds` for a missing stratum value (`getInt == -1`); `KFold` validates `folds > rowCount` but `StratifiedKFold` does not, producing empty test folds silently. Stratum var type is not checked (a DOUBLE strata column "works" via `getInt` truncation). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/split/KFold.java:44,52-66`, `StratifiedKFold.java:70-78` — the round-robin fold assignment is duplicated; the "stratified" version only stratifies approximately (concatenated shuffled strata are dealt round-robin, so per-fold class proportions can drift by one per stratum). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/RandIndex.java:26` — plain class with mutable `randIndex` field; not `Printable` like every other metric, no adjusted Rand index. [inconsistency]
- Tests `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ConfusionTest.java`, `ROCTest.java` — no test with `VarBinary` inputs, none with an empty class / all-positive scores (division by zero paths), none checks `Confusion.precision()` against a hand-computed value for the *named* positive class. [test]

#### ml.loss

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/HuberLossFunction.java:151-155,164-169` — `error`/`residualErrorScore` use `alpha` (a *quantile* in [0,1], per `withAlpha`) as the Huber *delta* threshold, while `gradient`/`scalarMinimizer` derive `rho` as the alpha-quantile of |residual|. The loss evaluated is therefore not the loss whose gradient is used. [bug] Fix: compute `rho` in `error` as well.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/HuberLossFunction.java:82-84` — weighted `scalarMinimizer(y, weight)` ignores `weight`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/HuberLossFunction.java:39-50` — mutable `alpha` with a `withAlpha` setter on an object that is shared by models (`equalOnParams` compares it) — every other configurable in the package is immutable/param-based. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/L2LossFunction.java:65-66` — `len` is computed as `min(y.size(), y_hat.size())` and then ignored (`VarDouble.from(y.size(), ...)`), so a shorter `y_hat` throws while `error()` at 71-72 silently truncates. Pick one behaviour (throw on size mismatch). [bug/inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/L2LossFunction.java:82,92` — `errorScore` returns `sqrt(Σ r² / (2n))`, i.e. RMSE/√2, which matches neither `error()` (r²/2) nor RMSE; the Javadoc in `LossFunction` should state exactly what "score" means. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/*.java` — parameter names `y_hat`, `r_bar` (snake_case) vs camelCase elsewhere; `Math.pow(x, 2)` in hot loops instead of `x*x`. [nit]

#### ml.common (distance, kernel, misc)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/distance/EuclideanDistance.java:48`, `Manhattan.java`, `MinkowskiDistance.java` — no missing-value policy: a NaN coordinate makes the whole distance NaN, and callers (`KMCluster`, which advertises `allowMissing=true`) compare with `>` so NaN silently loses every comparison. Either document "inputs must be complete" and validate, or implement the usual pairwise-complete scaling. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/distance/EuclideanDistance.java:48` — `x.sub(y).apply_(v -> v*v).sum()` allocates a temporary vector per distance call on the k-means/SMO hot path; a fused loop (as the `Frame` overload at 52-59 already does) avoids it. [design/perf]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/distance/Distance.java:63,96` — the `Frame` overloads iterate `df1.varNames()` and look up `df2` by name, so they silently ignore extra columns in `df2` and throw on a missing one; the two overloads can disagree with the `DArray` ones for the same data. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/*.java` (≈20 kernel classes) — each carries its own `equalOnParams`, `name`, `toString`, `newInstance` boilerplate; a generic parameterized kernel base would remove ~500 lines. `RBFKernel.fromSigma` uses `1/(2σ²)` while the constructor takes `gamma` directly; fine, but neither validates `gamma > 0`. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/kernel/Kernel.java` — `isLinear()` special-casing is checked at *every* `takeStep` in `BinarySMO`; kernels should expose a primal-update strategy or SMO should cache the flag once. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/svm/libsvm/SolverC.java:69` — `protected boolean unshrink; // XXX` and `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/LDA.java:175` `// TODO` (empty branch) are unresolved markers. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/common/Capabilities.java` — `inputs(1, 10_000, ...)` vs `inputs(1, 1_000_000, ...)` vs `Integer.MAX_VALUE` across models in this scope, with no rationale; the maximum-inputs check is effectively decorative. [inconsistency]

#### Cross-cutting

- Fluent parameter naming for the same concept differs across the scope: `runs` (KMCluster, BinaryLogistic maxIter), `maxRuns` (BinarySMO, PCA), `maxIt` (KMedoids), `maxIter` (RVM, logistic solvers); `c`/`"cost"`/`"c"`; `eps`/`tolerance`/`fitThreshold`; `intercept` as `Boolean` (linear) vs `Double` (logistic). [inconsistency]
- Instance `weights` are accepted by every `fit(df, weights, ...)` signature but honoured only by `WeightedLinearRegression` and `BinarySMO`; nothing tells the caller they were dropped. Add a `Capabilities.weights(boolean)` flag and validate. [design]
- Several models read mutable parameters at predict time (`kernel.get()` in BinarySMO/OneClassSvm, `intercept.get()` in BinaryLogistic, `center/standardize` in PCA): a parameter change after `fit` silently changes predictions instead of being rejected or ignored. [design]
- `getRandom()` returns a fresh `new Random(seed)` per call (`RegressionModel.java:343`); any code calling it more than once per fit (RVM) or in a loop gets correlated streams. Draw once per fit and pass it down. [bug]
- License/attribution: `libsvm` (BSD-3) and `BinarySMO` (Weka-shaped, GPL) carry only the project's Apache header. [legal]

#### Summary — top 5

1. **Logistic regression Newton solver is wrong with L2 penalty** — gradient omits `λw` and the line search evaluates the penalty at the wrong weights; both solvers also use `λ‖w‖` instead of `λ‖w‖²/2` in the objective and penalize the intercept. (`binarylogistic/BinaryLogisticNewton.java:133,152,177`, `BinaryLogisticIRLS.java:128,141`)
2. **k-means / k-medoids core loops have logic bugs** — empty-cluster repair in `KMCluster` never runs (NaN centroids), k-means++ uses D instead of D², PAM scores the new assignment against old medoids, `pamSwap` never skips current medoids, and the ALTERNATE method never swaps because it looks clusters up by the wrong key. (`km/KMCluster.java:293-327`, `km/KMClusterInit.java:63`, `km/KMedoids.java:181-193,317,461`)
3. **LDA silently drops the first class and projects new data in the wrong space** — `levels().subList(1, …)` assumes a `"?"` level that is not there; standardization is applied twice in `fit` and with the *new* frame's statistics in `transform`; eigenvectors are of `S_B S_W⁻¹` instead of `S_W⁻¹ S_B`. (`analysis/LDA.java:88-124,171,205`)
4. **LIBSVM wrapper defects** — class weights are discarded (`weightLabel` overwritten with ±1), the kernel cache is 8× the configured size, `withDistributions=true` on a 2-class problem throws AIOOBE, and there is no LIBSVM attribution. (`svm/libsvm/ProblemInfo.java:151-166`, `SvcKernelMatrix.java:38`, `svm/SvmClassifier.java:169-173`)
5. **BinarySMO** — linear-kernel prediction indexes weights by compacted position instead of `_sparseIndices`, `prob=true` NPEs with a linear kernel and otherwise calibrates on support vectors only, prediction copies the whole input matrix per row, and the one-vs-all path writes to a level that does not exist. (`svm/BinarySMO.java:236,270-277,588,603`)

#### ml.model.rvm (RVMRegression, sampled)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:680` — `initializeAlphaBeta` calls `parent.getRandom()` *inside* the per-element lambda; `getRandom()` returns `new Random(seed)` each time, so with a fixed seed every `alpha_i` gets the identical value (and with seed 0, a fresh unseeded RNG per element). The `random` obtained at `coreFit:402` is never handed to the method implementations. [bug] Pass the single `Random` down.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:578-584,689-695` — posterior covariance is obtained by explicit inversion `t.lu().inv()` with `catch (IllegalArgumentException)` → `qr().inv()` fallback; the matrix `βΦ'Φ + A` is SPD, so Cholesky solve (no explicit inverse, exception-free SPD check) is both faster and stabler. Exceptions as control flow. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:586-595` — update equations `α_i = γ_i/m_i²`, `β = (N−Σγ)/‖y−Φm‖²` match Tipping (2001); `m_i = 0` yields `α=∞` which is then pruned — acceptable but relies on IEEE semantics; document. [ok/nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:643,1225` — bare `RuntimeException("All vectors pruned.")` / `IllegalStateException` for algorithmic dead-ends; the model should report `converged=false` or throw a typed exception. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java` (1293 lines) — one file holds the model, `RvmRunInfo`, the `FeatureProvider` hierarchy, three algorithm implementations (`EvidenceApproximation`, `FastTipping`, `FastOnline`) and `PhiCache`. Split into a package like `svm/libsvm`. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/rvm/RVMRegression.java:105,110` — `maxIter`/`maxFailures` default 10 000 with no validator; `fitThreshold`/`alphaThreshold` have no positivity check. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/model/RegressionModel.java:343-345`, `ClassifierModel.java:407-409`, `ClusteringModel.java:208-210` — `getRandom()` semantics differ: classifiers test `seed.hasDefaultValue()`, regressors/clusterers test `seed.get()==0` (so `seed.set(0)` explicitly is unseeded for one family and seeded for the other); `KMedoids:127` bypasses both. [inconsistency]

#### ml.analysis (PCA, LDA)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/LDA.java:204-205` — `targetLevels = var.levels().subList(1, ...)` assumes level 0 is the missing marker, but `VarNominal.levels()` does *not* contain `"?"`, so the **first real class is dropped** from the analysis (3-class iris becomes a 2-class LDA). [bug] Use `levels()` as is (and reject rows with missing target).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/LDA.java:124` — solves the eigenproblem of `S_b · S_w⁻¹` instead of `S_w⁻¹ · S_b`. Same eigenvalues, but the eigenvectors of `S_b S_w⁻¹` are `S_w·v`, not the discriminant directions `v`; projections are wrong unless the matrices commute. [bug] Use `swi.mm(sb)` (or solve the generalized symmetric problem).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/LDA.java:88-92,112-113` — with `scaling=true` the data is standardized (`mxx`), then `vmean/vstd` are recomputed on the standardized data and `xc = (mxx − vmean)/vstd` standardizes *again*; with `scaling=false`, `xc` is standardized anyway, so the flag changes nothing in `fit`. `transform:170-171` then standardizes with the *test* frame's own mean/std instead of the training `vmean/vstd`, and only when `scaling=true`. Fit and transform are on different scales. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/LDA.java:113,118` — `S_w` is computed as the *total* scatter `X_cᵀX_c/n` (not within-class Σ_c Σ_{x∈c}(x−μ_c)(x−μ_c)ᵀ) and `S_b` averages class-mean outer products unweighted by class size. Directions coincide with proper LDA only because `S_t = S_w + S_b`; eigenvalues (and the printed "percent") are not the standard ones, and class-size weighting is lost. [design] Document or implement the textbook scatter matrices.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/LDA.java:165` — allows `k == targetLevels.size()`; LDA has at most `K−1` non-trivial directions. `k` is not checked against `inputNames.length`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/LDA.java:60` — parameter name `"Scaling data or not"` is a sentence, not a name (shows up in `fullName()`). `fit` returns `void` while `PCA.fit` returns `this`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:69` — `center` parameter is registered under the name `"scaling"`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:59,64` — `eps` and `maxRuns` are declared, documented and printed but never used (eigendecomposition is direct). [dead]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:115,124` — standardization uses `std1d(0, 0)` (population sd, ddof=0) while eigenvalues are divided by `n−1` (sample covariance): with `standardize=true` the eigenvalues no longer sum to `p`. A constant column gives `sd=0` → NaN everywhere. [inconsistency/bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:120-125` — uses the general (non-symmetric) `eig()` on the symmetric scatter matrix and takes `real()`; a symmetric solver or an SVD of `X` avoids spurious tiny imaginary parts and negative eigenvalues, and is what every PCA implementation does. Eigenvector signs are not normalised (e.g. largest component positive), so results differ from R/sklearn only by sign and tests become brittle. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:108` — `fit` uses `df.darray()` (all columns) but `transform` uses `df.mapVars(inputNames)`; consistent only because `inputNames` is every column. `k` is not validated against `inputVars`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:213-219` — `toContent`/`toFullContent` call `toSummary(options)` (the `Printable` default without a printer) instead of `toSummary(printer, options)`; the printer is silently dropped. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/analysis/PCA.java:88-101`, `LDA.java:75-80` — getters expose internal `DArray`s. [design]
- Tests: `LDATest` (55 lines) cannot detect the dropped class or the wrong eigenvector orientation; a test against a known 3-class solution (e.g. iris vs. R's `MASS::lda` scalings, up to sign/scale) is missing. `PCATest` has no `standardize=true` case with a constant column. [test]

#### ml.loss

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/L1LossFunction.java:49-51` and `HuberLossFunction.java:82-84` — `scalarMinimizer(Var y, Var weight)` ignores `weight` and returns the unweighted median/Huber estimate; boosting with instance weights silently uses wrong leaf values. [bug] Weighted median.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/HuberLossFunction.java:151-170` — `error()`/`residualErrorScore()` use `alpha` (a *quantile* in [0,1], default 0.25) directly as the Huber threshold δ (`a < alpha`), while `gradient()`/`scalarMinimizer()` correctly derive δ = quantile_α(|r|). The reported error is therefore of a different loss than the one being optimised. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/HuberLossFunction.java:39-50` — mutable `alpha` with a `withAlpha` setter on an object that participates in `equalOnParams` and is stored inside serialized models; every other loss is immutable. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/L2LossFunction.java:65-66,71` — `len` computed and unused in `gradient`, used in `error`; `errorScore` returns `sqrt(Σ/(2n))` (RMSE/√2), which matches neither `error` (½r²) nor RMSE and is undocumented. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/KDevianceLossFunction.java:107-124` — `gradient`/`error`/`errorScore`/`residualErrorScore` throw `IllegalStateException`; the interface contract is violated rather than narrowed. Use `UnsupportedOperationException` or split the interface (this loss is only usable by the k-class boosting code). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/KDevianceLossFunction.java:131`, `HuberLossFunction.java:178` — `Integer.valueOf(k).equals(...)`, `Double.valueOf(alpha).equals(...)` instead of `==`/`Double.compare`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/loss/LossFunction.java:37` — interface mixes regression losses, a classification deviance and a tree-split score (`RTreeLossFunction`) under one name; `L2LossFunction` implements both. [design]
- Tests: no `tst/rapaio/ml/loss` directory at all; the weighted-minimizer and Huber `error` bugs are untested. [test]

#### Summary (top 5)

1. **LDA is wrong twice**: drops the first class (`LDA.java:205`, `levels()` has no `"?"`) and diagonalises `S_b S_w⁻¹` instead of `S_w⁻¹ S_b` (`LDA.java:124`), plus fit/transform scale mismatch. Results are not LDA.
2. **k-means empty-cluster repair never runs and corrupts centroids** (`KMCluster.java:293-347`): wrong emptiness test, wrong collision check, recompute with stale assignment → NaN centroids; `MWKMeans` has no handling at all. k-means++ also samples by `D` instead of `D²`.
3. **Kernel formulas**: `SigmoidKernel` uses `atan` not `tanh`; `Cauchy`, `Log`, `Power`, `GeneralizedStudentT`, `Wave` use the squared distance where the distance is required; `Spherical`/`Circular` have the support test inverted. Anything using these kernels (SVM, RVM) trains on a different kernel than documented.
4. **Penalised logistic regression**: Newton omits `−λw` from the gradient and evaluates the line search on the wrong vector (`BinaryLogisticNewton.java:152,177`); both solvers penalise the intercept and report `λ‖w‖` instead of `λ‖w‖²/2`; `EXPECTED_LOG_VAR` init uses `min` for `max`; `l1penalty` is a no-op; density columns are hard-coded.
5. **Evaluation metrics mislabel binary data**: `Confusion` rows/cols are swapped for `VarBinary` (`getInt`=1 for true vs `levels()=[true,false]`), F1/precision/recall/MCC/AUC have no zero-division guards, `ROC` counts rows it never sweeps, `ClusterSilhouette` gives singletons `s=1` and mis-signs similarities. Also worth fixing soon: `SvmClassifier` density double-write and class-weight label overwrite, missing LIBSVM attribution, `KernelCache` 900 MB allocation at n=10 000, RVM identical random alphas.

### 2.6 ml.eval, ml.loss, ml.common

# Review: rapaio.ml.eval, rapaio.ml.loss, rapaio.ml.common (excl. kernel)

Root: /home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib

#### ml.eval.metric

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:160` [bug] `fpr = fp / n` unguarded; when there are no negatives (`n == 0`) every fpr is NaN and `auc` (line 177, `/ (p*n)`) is NaN. tpr is guarded at line 161 but fpr is not. Fix: guard `n > 0 ? fp/n : 0` and define AUC as NaN explicitly with a comment, or throw when p==0 || n==0.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:142` [bug] `prev = Double.MIN_VALUE` is used as an "impossible" sentinel while counting distinct thresholds; MIN_VALUE is the smallest positive double, not a sentinel, so a score exactly equal to it is silently merged with the sentinel. Use `Double.NaN` + `!=` on NaN, or `Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY` as in the second loop (line 151).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:140` [bug] `VarRefSort` sorts all rows including those with missing score; the missing rows are only skipped inside the loop. Depending on comparator NaN placement they are harmless, but `len` counted in the first loop starts at 1 and adds one per distinct value, producing exactly `distinct+1` rows, which relies on the very first score differing from MIN_VALUE. Fragile; compute `len` from a set of distinct non-missing values.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:69` [design] `ROC.from(score, actual, predict)` defines the positive class as "prediction equals actual", i.e. a ROC of *correctness*, which is not a standard ROC and is easy to confuse with the one-vs-rest overload at line 103. Rename (e.g. `fromCorrectness`) or drop.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:49-52` [inconsistency] public `static final String threshold/fpr/tpr/acc` are lowercase constants; rest of the codebase uses UPPER_SNAKE for constants (cf. `Accuracy.ID`).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:47` [design] ROC is not a `ClassifierMetric` and its AUC cannot be plugged into `ClassifierEvaluation`. An `AUC` metric adapter would be the obvious consumer.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:188` [nit] `findRowForThreshold` returns the first row with `th <= value` by linear scan; data is sorted descending so a binary search would do, and the Javadoc (if any) should state "first threshold not greater than value".
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ROC.java:213-224` [nit] Ten copy-pasted `k * rowCount/10` lines; a loop `for k in 0..10` is shorter and avoids duplicate rows when rowCount < 11.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:139-149` [bug] Binary case silently treats the *first* level (`levels().get(0)`) as the positive class. For `VarBinary` that is `false`, so `tp` is "correctly predicted false". Nothing in the API lets the caller choose the positive label. Add `from(actual, predict, positiveLabel)` or document loudly.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:145-149` [bug] `mcc`, `precision`, `recall`, `f1`, `g` divide by zero when a row/column is empty (e.g. classifier predicts a single class) and become NaN; no guard and no documentation. sklearn-style convention is 0 with a warning.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:137` [bug] `cmProbability.div_(completeCases)` with `completeCases == 0` yields a NaN matrix although `acc` is guarded at line 132.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:108-118` [design] Requires `actual` and `predict` to have *identical* level lists in the same order. A prediction var built with a superset/reordered dictionary (common when a fold lacks a class) is rejected, and there is no label-based remapping. Map by label instead of by int index.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:85-87` [inconsistency] `factors = actual.levels(true)` (with the "?" prefix) and then `factors.size() - 1` and `factors.get(i + 1)` everywhere (lines 86, 184-233). Using `levels()` directly removes ~15 off-by-one adjustments.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:106` [nit] Error message "Predicted values variable must be nominal." omits "or binary" unlike line 103.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:46` [design] No per-class precision/recall/F1 nor macro/micro/weighted averaging for the multiclass case; only binary scalars exist. This is the most commonly requested classification report and it is missing.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Confusion.java:421` [design] `frequencyMatrix()`/`probabilityMatrix()` expose the internal mutable `DArray`; caller can corrupt `acc` consistency. Return a copy or document.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Accuracy.java:56-57` [bug] No missing-value handling: `actual.getLabel(i)` returns "?" and is compared with the predicted label; a missing actual always counts as a miss and is still included in the denominator. Confusion excludes incomplete cases; the two disagree.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Accuracy.java:32` [nit] `extends AbstractClassifierMetric implements ClassifierMetric` — the interface is already implemented by the abstract class.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/Accuracy.java:33` vs `LogLoss.java:48` [inconsistency] `public static final String ID` vs `private static final String NAME` for the same concept.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/LogLoss.java:63` [bug] `result.firstDensity().getDouble(i, actual.getLabel(i))` with a missing actual label passes "?" as a column name -> exception (or wrong column) rather than skipping the row.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/LogLoss.java:63` [nit] Clipping to `[eps, 1-eps]` is fine, but the upper clip is unnecessary for the loss (log(1)=0) and `eps` is not validated to be in (0, 0.5).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/AbstractClassifierMetric.java:35` [design] Classifier metrics are stateful (`protected ClassifierScore score` mutated by `compute`, which returns `this`) while regression metrics return an immutable `RegressionScore`. A shared metric instance used from two threads races; `ClassifierEvaluationResult.appendRun` only avoids this because it computes under a lock. Make `compute` return `ClassifierScore` and drop `getScore()`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/ClassifierMetric.java:36` vs `RegressionMetric.java:36-38` [inconsistency] `RegressionMetric` has a `compute(Var, Var)` overload; `ClassifierMetric` has none, so metrics cannot be computed from two label vars without a `ClassifierResult`.

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/MAE.java:57` [nit] Constructor parameter `String... targetVarNames` is never used.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/MAE.java:58` [bug] Validates `varCount` equality but not `rowCount` equality; a shorter `fit` frame throws AIOOBE deep inside, a longer one is silently truncated.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/metric/MAE.java:66-80` [nit] `count` is a `double` and `totalCount` an `int` accumulating the same quantity.

#### ml.eval.split

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/split/Split.java:34` [bug] Convenience constructor builds `trainWeights` with `VarDouble.fill(trainDf.rowCount())` which fills with **0.0** (see `VarDouble.fill(int)` -> `new VarDouble(rows, rows, 0)`), while `testWeights` uses `fill(n, 1)`. Any model trained through this constructor gets all-zero weights. Fix: `VarDouble.fill(trainDf.rowCount(), 1)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/split/KFold.java:72-73` [bug] When `weights == null` the `Split` is created with `null` weights instead of unit weights; downstream `m.fit(df, weights, target)` callers must special-case null. Default to `VarDouble.fill(n, 1)` (via the 4-arg constructor once fixed).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/split/KFold.java:44` [inconsistency] Validates `folds > rowCount` but not `folds < 2` nor `rounds < 1`; `StratifiedKFold` validates nothing at all (folds > rowCount yields empty test folds -> downstream division by zero).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/split/StratifiedKFold.java:63` [bug] `rows.get(df.getInt(i, strataName))` throws AIOOBE for a missing strata value (`getInt` returns -1) and silently misbehaves for a non-nominal strata var. Validate the var type and either skip or bucket missing rows.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/split/StratifiedKFold.java:65-78` [design] Correct stratification (per-stratum shuffle, concatenate, round-robin) but the concatenation order is fixed by dictionary order, so fold 0 always gets the surplus of the first stratum in every round; a second shuffle of the strata order per round would remove this bias.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/split/StratifiedKFold.java:61,70` [nit] `.toList()` on one line and `.collect(Collectors.toList())` on the next for the same purpose.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/split/KFold.java:59` [ok] Every row appears in exactly one test fold per round (round-robin over a shuffled permutation); the `Random` is the one passed in, not a global. Good.
- [test] `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/ml/eval/split/KFoldTest.java` exists; there is no `StratifiedKFoldTest` (class balance per fold, missing strata, rounds > 1) and no test for `Split` default weights (which would have caught the 0-weights bug).

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClassifierEvaluation.java:104` [inconsistency] Param is named `seed` in code but registered as `"random"`; every other param name matches the field.

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClassifierEvaluation.java:121-124` [ok] A fresh `model.newInstance()` per split — no shared model between threads. Note that the model's own internal seed (if any) is shared across folds, which is fine.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClassifierEvaluation.java:123` [design] Predicting on the whole train fold with `withDistributions=true` for every split doubles the cost even when the caller only wants test scores; make train-score computation optional.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClassifierEvaluationResult.java:105-107,118-120` [design] Metrics are computed inside the result's lock, serialising the (possibly expensive) metric evaluation across threads and coupling the result object to metric computation. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClassifierEvaluationResult.java:109-112,123-126` [design] The whole scores frame is re-sorted and deep-copied after *every* appended run (O(k^2) copies); results already arrive in order via `streamingOrdered`. Sort once at the end (or not at all).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClassifierEvaluationResult.java:68-69` [bug] Metric columns are keyed by `metric.getName()`; two metrics with the same name (e.g. `Accuracy.newMetric(true)` and `Accuracy.newMetric(false)` both named "Accuracy") collide in the frame and in `getMeanTestScore(name)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClassifierEvaluationResult.java:139` [nit] Header "CV score in training data" precedes a table that contains both test and train rows.

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClassifierEvaluationResult.java:71` [nit] `SolidFrame.byVars(vars).copy()` — the copy is redundant.

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/RegressionEval.java:51,68,53,94` vs `ClassifierEvaluation.java:51,82,56,110` [inconsistency] Naming diverges everywhere between the two twins: `RegressionEval`/`ClassifierEvaluation` (results are both `*EvaluationResult`), `df`/`data`, `newEval()`/`eval(...)`+`cv(...)`, default metrics `[]`/`[Accuracy]`, threads default `nproc-1`/`1`, executor `FixedThreadPool`/`WorkStealingPool`, seed param name `"seed"`/`"random"`. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/RegressionEval.java:94` [design] Empty default metric list means `newEval().run()` produces a scores frame with only `round`/`fold`; default to RMSE.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/RegressionEvaluationResult.java:105-108,118-121` [design] Same per-append full re-sort + deep copy as the classifier result (O(k^2)).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClusterSilhouette.java:206` [bug] `s = (b - a) / max(a, b)` is applied regardless of `similarity`. When `similarity == true`, `b` is the *largest* mean similarity to another cluster (lines 187-191) and `a` the mean within-cluster similarity; a good clustering has `a > b`, so the silhouette sign must be flipped (`(a - b)/max`). As written, similarity mode returns negated silhouettes.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClusterSilhouette.java:178,206` [bug] Singleton cluster: `a[row] = 0` when the instance is alone, so `s = b/b = 1`. Rousseeuw's definition (and sklearn) sets `s = 0` for singletons. Add the special case.

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClusterSilhouette.java:69-79` [nit] `Objects::nonNull` validators with `null` defaults, so a freshly built object is invalid by construction; `similarity` should default to `false`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClusterSilhouette.java:104-110` [design] `getClusterScores()`/`getScores()` hand out the internal arrays; callers can mutate results.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClusterSilhouette.java:233,245` [nit] `-1 * Double.compare(...)` -> `Comparator.reverseOrder()`/`Double.compare(b, a)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/ClusterSilhouette.java:302` [nit] `String.format("%5.2f")` is locale-dependent; rest of the class uses `Format.floatFlex`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/RandIndex.java:58-59` [bug] Missing labels compare as `"?" == "?"`, i.e. all missing rows form one implicit class/cluster. Reject or skip missing.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/ml/eval/RandIndex.java:49` [nit] Size mismatch throws `IllegalStateException`; everywhere else it is `IllegalArgumentException`.
