- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/atom/AtomRegistry.java:60-66` — `getSerializationHandler` iterates `registry.keySet()` and returns the **first** key with `isAssignableFrom`; with a `HashMap` the iteration order is arbitrary, so if two registered types are related (e.g. `Storage` and a subclass) the handler chosen is nondeterministic. Exact-match lookup should be tried first. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/atom/AtomRegistry.java:27-29,44-48` — `rapaio.io.atom` hard-codes `rapaio.darray.*` handlers: the generic I/O layer depends on a concrete data package, and every user of `AtomRegistry` loads darray classes. [design] Fix: `ServiceLoader<AtomSerialization>` or registration from the darray side.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/atom/AtomSerialization.java:31-34` — reads `T` from `getGenericSuperclass()`: throws `ClassCastException` for any subclass that is itself generic or two levels deep (`class X<T> extends AtomSerialization<T>`) or an anonymous class. Prefer passing `Class<T>` to the constructor. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/atom/SaveAtomHandler.java:29` — `SaveAtomHandler<T>.saveAtom(Object atom, ...)` takes `Object`, not `T`; the type parameter is unused, so every handler does an unchecked cast. [inconsistency] Fix: `void saveAtom(T atom, AtomOutputStream out)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/atom/TextAtomProtocol.java:37,44` — inner class in the **Text** protocol is named `BinaryAtomOutputStream`; the protocol is write-only (no `AtomInputStream`), so text output cannot be read back. `new FileWriter(file)` / `OutputStreamWriter(out)` use the default charset (should be explicit UTF-8). Arrays are dumped with `Arrays.toString` (unparseable for large data). [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/atom/AtomContext.java:34-37` — constructor accepts a `null` map silently; `with` mutates and returns `this` (fluent but not immutable). Fine, but document. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/atom/AtomInputStream.java` / `AtomOutputStream.java` — no `saveLong/readLong`, `saveBoolean`, `saveShort` even though `VarLong`/`VarBinary` exist in `rapaio.data`; any handler for those has to widen to `double` or `int`. [design]

#### io — JavaIO, Csv, ArffPersistence

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/JavaIO.java:43-47,53-57` — `FileInputStream`/`ObjectInputStream`/`FileOutputStream`/`ObjectOutputStream` are never closed (no try-with-resources): file-handle leak and, on write, buffered data may never be flushed to disk (`ObjectOutputStream` buffers; `writeObject` without `close()/flush()` can leave a truncated file). [bug] Fix: try-with-resources on both.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/JavaIO.java:43-47` — `ObjectInputStream.readObject()` on an arbitrary file with no `ObjectInputFilter` is a classic deserialization-attack vector when the file comes from a user. [bug]/security. Fix: `in.setObjectInputFilter(ObjectInputFilter.Config.createFilter("rapaio.**;java.base/*;!*"))` or deprecate in favour of the atom protocol.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:242-245` — `while (!keepRows.get().test(allRowsNum)) { reader.readLine(); allRowsNum++; }` never checks for EOF: with `keepRows = IntRule.none()` or a rule that rejects all rows in the file, `readLine()` returns `null` forever and the loop spins indefinitely. [bug] Fix: break on `null`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:389` — in `clean`, the escape look-ahead guard is `len < tok.length() - 1` where `len` is the **output** index; after one unescape `len < i`, so at `i == tok.length()-1` the guard is still true and `tok.charAt(i + 1)` throws `StringIndexOutOfBoundsException` (e.g. `escapeChar='\\'`, token `\"x\`). [bug] Fix: `i < tok.length() - 1`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:232` — `new InputStreamReader(inputStream)` uses the JVM default charset; there is no `charset` parameter although every other aspect is parametrised. Same in `write` (line 422) and `ArffPersistence.java:71`. [inconsistency] Fix: add `ValueParam<Charset>` defaulting to UTF-8.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:249,331-348` — parsing is line-based (`readLine`), so a quoted field containing a newline (legal RFC 4180) is split across two records. [bug]/limitation. Document or implement a char-level reader.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:333` — tokenization treats `"` as a quote character **regardless** of the `quotes` param (`quotes` only controls stripping in `clean`), so with `quotes=false` a stray `"` inside a value (e.g. `5" pipe`) swallows the following separators. [bug] Fix: honour `quotes.get()` in `parseLine`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:328,359` — a line ending with the separator (`a,b,`) yields 2 fields, not 3 (loop exits when `start == length`); the missing trailing field is later filled with `"?"` only because `read()` pads short rows, so `parseLine` alone is wrong for callers using it directly (it is public). Also an empty line becomes a full row of NAs instead of being skipped. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:374-400` — `clean` strips whitespace **before** removing quotes and **again after**, so `" a "` (quoted, intentionally padded) becomes `a`; RFC semantics say quoted content is verbatim. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:378-383` — leading and trailing `"` are stripped independently, so an unbalanced `"abc` becomes `abc` silently. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:432,445` — `new DecimalFormat("0.###...")` uses the **default locale**: in `ro_RO`/`de_DE` doubles are written with a decimal comma and the file cannot be read back by the same class (which uses `Double.parseDouble`). Also `INT`, `LONG`, `BINARY`, `INSTANT` columns are written via `getDouble` → `long` loses precision above 2^53 and instants become epoch numbers. [bug] Fix: `DecimalFormat` with `DecimalFormatSymbols.getInstance(Locale.ROOT)` (or `Double.toString`), and write `getLabel` for non-double types.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:443,454-468` — with `quotes=false` (default) string labels containing the separator, `"` or a newline are written unquoted/unescaped (only `"` gets the escape char), producing a file the reader cannot parse. [bug] Fix: quote when the label contains separator, quote, CR or LF.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:422-451` — `PrintWriter` swallows `IOException`s; `checkError()` is never consulted, so a full disk produces a truncated file with no exception. [bug] Fix: use `BufferedWriter` directly (throws) or check `writer.checkError()`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:424,428` — `df.varNames()` is called twice per header column; header names are not escaped/quoted. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:169,174` — fields `keepRows`/`keepCols` are registered under the param names `"skipRows"`/`"skipCols"` — opposite meaning in any name-based API (`ParamSet` copy/print). [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:86-94,136` — `DEFAULT_TYPES` is a shared **mutable** `ArrayList` handed to every `Csv` instance's `ListParam` default; if `ListParam` keeps the reference, `csv.defaultTypes.add(...)` on one instance alters the default for all future instances. [bug] (verify `ListParam` copies) Fix: `List.of(...)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:206-215` — `readUrl` closes the stream only on success (`is.close()` not in `finally`); the `BufferedReader` in `read` closes it anyway, so harmless but misleading. `read(File)` wraps `IOException` in a `RuntimeException` while `read(InputStream)`/`read(Class,String)` throw checked `IOException`: inconsistent error contract across overloads. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:569,601` — type-promotion fallback catches `TextParserException|IllegalArgumentException` in one place and `Exception` in the other; a `NumberFormatException` (which *is* an IAE) is fine but any bug in a parser is silently turned into "try next type". [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:521` — `configParser(Serializable parser)` takes `Serializable` and casts to `Parser<X>` per type: eight unchecked casts; `Parser<?>` would at least be honest. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/Csv.java:594-596` — on type fallback, all previous raw values are re-added from `text`, but `text` itself is kept until the last type: every column of unknown type stores its raw strings twice until parsing finishes (memory ×2 for large text-like files). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/ArffPersistence.java:114` — recognises `real`, `isNumeric`, `integer` but **not** `numeric`, the most common ARFF numeric type; such attributes fall through to the nominal branch and are parsed as categories. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/ArffPersistence.java:181` — `fullTrim`: `in.endsWith("'") || in.startsWith("\"")` should be `endsWith("\"")`; a value like `"abc` loses its last char and `abc"` keeps the quote. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/ArffPersistence.java:103-107,152` — `Pattern.compile` per attribute line, `m.find()` result unchecked (throws `IllegalStateException` on a lone `'`); data rows split with `split(",")` so quoted values containing commas break; `date`, `string`, sparse `{...}` rows and `?` inside quotes are unsupported. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/io/ArffPersistence.java:137-147` — if an attribute is neither `VarDouble` nor `VarNominal` (cannot happen today) `newvectors` and `names` get out of sync; brittle. [nit]

#### printer — Format, TextTable, Printer/Printable, opt, standard, idea

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/Format.java:32,44,56,68,86,104` — every formatter is `new DecimalFormat()` with the **default locale**: on `de_DE`/`ro_RO` output uses `,` as decimal separator and `.` as grouping (`1.234,567`). `TextTable.floatString` then anchors on `'.'` (see below) and mis-aligns, `Csv.write` produces unreadable files, and any test comparing printed text is locale-fragile. [bug] Fix: `new DecimalFormat("...", DecimalFormatSymbols.getInstance(Locale.ROOT))`, `setGroupingUsed(false)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/Format.java:31-65` — `floatShort/Medium/Long(double)` print `NaN` as the locale NaN symbol (`�`/`NaN`) and infinities as `∞`, while the `floatFlex*` variants special-case them to `"?"` / `Double.toString`. Inconsistent within one class. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/Format.java:39-40` etc. — a new `DecimalFormat` is allocated per formatted value (`floatShort(double)` calls `floatShort()`); in `TextTable` this is per cell. `DecimalFormat` construction is expensive (pattern parse + symbols lookup). [design] Fix: `ThreadLocal<DecimalFormat>` or `NumberFormat` instances cached per pattern.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/Format.java:144` — `String.format("%10.2e", pvalue)` is also default-locale dependent and pads to width 10 for no reason inside a table cell. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/TextTable.java:136,150,163` — `text.indexOf('.') > -0` is `> 0`: a value like `.5` (index 0) is treated as "no decimal point" and not anchored. Author clearly meant `>= 0`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/TextTable.java:388-389` — `computeSplitText`: `lastCol` starts at `headerCols`; if `headerCols >= cols` (table is all header columns) the `while` never executes and the method returns `""`, so a wide all-header table prints nothing. [bug] Fix: guard `headerCols >= cols` → raw text.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/TextTable.java:397-402,412` — width accounting ignores the one-space separator appended per column (`append(' ')`), so a "fit to console width" table overflows by `cols` characters and wraps in a real terminal. [bug] Fix: count `finalLen[c] + 1`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/TextTable.java:207-249` — `set(row, col, value, align, anchor)` with `value == null` and an anchor NPEs at `value.length()`; `textCenter(r,c,null)` sets `center=null,left=null,right=null` and then `computeFinalTexts` NPEs at `center[i][j].length()`. [nit] Fix: `Objects.requireNonNullElse(value, "")`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/TextTable.java:443-459` — `computeMergeText` builds rows with `String +=` in a triple loop: O(rows·cols·len) copying. Use `StringBuilder[]`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/TextTable.java:135-141,148-155,161-168` — `floatString`, `floatFlexLong`, `floatMedium` are the same 6 lines three times; `floatFlex` correctly delegates to `floatString`, the other two do not. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/opt/POpt.java:27` + `NamedParam.java:26-29` — `POpt implements Serializable` but its superclass `NamedParam` is not `Serializable` and has no no-arg constructor, and holds a `Function<S,V>` lambda that is not serializable either. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/opt/NamedParamSet.java:92` — `ref.defaults.get(p.getName())` NPEs if a `NamedParam` that was never registered (constructed ad hoc with a new name) is queried. [nit]

#### datasets

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/datasets/Datasets.java:56-169` — error handling differs per method: some throw checked `IOException` (`loadPearsonHeightDataset`, `loadSpamBase`, `loadHousing`, `loadLifeScience`, `loadSonar`), others wrap in `RuntimeException`, and `loadMushrooms`/`loasSAheart` throw `new RuntimeException(e.getMessage())` **dropping the cause**. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/datasets/TabularDataset.java:117,126-130` — `batchSize <= 0` → `ArithmeticException` from `floorDiv` (or infinite `hasNext`); `next()` does not check `hasNext()` and returns empty arrays past the end instead of `NoSuchElementException`. [nit]

[test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/util/DoublesTest.java` (13 tests) — no NaN cases for `equals/min/max/argmin/argmax`, no `-0.0` for `newFill`, no `variance` with `len==1`, no test of `toString` for `length > 20`. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/util/IntOpenHashSetTest.java` (3), `Int2IntOpenHashMapTest.java` (2) — no test for `Integer.MIN_VALUE` as key/value, `allocation ∈ {0,1}`, `LINEAR` probing, `clear()` then re-add, `values()` with `MIN_VALUE`, heavy-load collision behaviour (the infinite-loop risk). [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/util/hash/Murmur3Test.java` — validates `murmur3A(byte[])`/`murmur3F` against reference vectors (good) but not `murmur3A(int)`, `murmur3A(double)` or `murmur3A(CharSequence)` (UTF-8 surrogate-pair path) against the byte[] implementation. [test]
- No tests for `Floats`, `LongArrays`, `TArrays`, `Pair` (would catch `update(Pair)` no-op), `DoublePair`, `IntRule`, `Hardware`, all three `*Comparators`, `IntIterable`, `rapaio.util.parralel.*`. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/util/time/PrettyTimeIntervalTest.java` (2 tests) — only aligned boundaries; no test for `getInstantList` with unaligned `end`, month/year edges (Feb 29, Dec 31 → Jan 1), or `_8_HOUR` bucket boundaries. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/io/CsvTest.java` (9 tests) — `parseLine`/`clean` are covered for a few escape/quote combinations, but there is **no write→read round-trip**, no non-`en` `Locale` test, no trailing-separator line, no empty line, no `keepRows = none()` (hangs), no multi-byte charset, no `startRow/endRow`, no `template`. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/io/AtomTest.java` (1), `atom/BasicSerializationTest.java` (2) — happy path only; no truncated-stream, no negative/huge length, no `TextAtomProtocol`, no `AtomRegistry.register` of a user type, no `>65535`-byte string. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/printer/TextTableTest.java` (2), `opt/NamedParamSetTest.java` (1) — no `Format` tests (locale!), no `computeSplitText`/`computeMergeText` width tests, no `headerCols == cols`, no `POpts` serialization test (would fail). No tests for `StandardPrinter`, `ImageTools`, `idea/*`. [test]
- No `ArffPersistence` test (deprecated but still shipped and used by `Datasets.loadSonar`). [test]
- No `JavaIO` test. [test]

#### Summary — top 5

1. **Locale-dependent number formatting everywhere** (`printer/Format.java` all `new DecimalFormat()`, `Csv.write` line 432, `DoublePair.toString`, `Format.pValue`): output changes with the JVM default locale, CSV files written on a `de_DE` machine cannot be read back, and `TextTable` decimal alignment breaks. Fix once with `DecimalFormatSymbols.getInstance(Locale.ROOT)`.
2. **`--enable-preview` and `java.sql` are shipped in the module/pom without being used** (`pom.xml:93,152,156`, `module-info.java:7`), forcing every consumer to run with `--enable-preview`; plus the javadoc plugin's duplicate `<additionalOptions>` and the `${argLine}`-without-default surefire config. Build hygiene issues that affect all users.
3. **Open-addressing hash structures are unsafe** (`IntOpenHashSet`, `Int2IntOpenHashMap`): cumulative quadratic probing on non-power-of-two tables can loop forever, `Integer.MIN_VALUE` cannot be stored in the set (silently), `values()` drops `MIN_VALUE` values, and `allocation ≤ 1` divides by zero.
4. **I/O robustness**: `Csv.read` infinite loop with a rejecting `keepRows` (line 242), `clean()` index bug (line 389), quotes handled in tokenizer regardless of `quotes` param, unquoted separators on write; `BinaryAtomProtocol` ignores `readNBytes` result, trusts length prefixes, uses unbuffered file streams and `writeUTF`; `JavaIO` leaks streams and deserializes without a filter; `Datasets` depends on a test-scoped artifact via the system class loader.
5. **Concrete small bugs worth a one-line fix each**: `Ints.equals` (`len - aStart`), `Pair.update(Pair)` no-op, `Ints/Doubles.parallelQuickSort(x, y, ...)` missing `else`, `Ints.quickSortIndirect(perm, x)` using `x.length`, `TextTable` `> -0`, `Datasets.loadRandom` `== -1`, `ArffPersistence.fullTrim` `startsWith("\"")`, `Hardware` MAC branch reading the first `sysctl` line, `StandardPrinter.draw` off-EDT busy-wait.

### 2.9 graphics

# Review: rapaio.graphics (base, opt, plot, plot.artist) + tests

Root: `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics`. Analysis only, no code changed.

#### rapaio.graphics.opt

##### GOpts / GOpt (option system)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:79` — option key is misspelled `"hAling"` (should be `"hAlign"`). Harmless at runtime because the key is only compared with itself, but it shows up in `toString()` and would break any future by-name lookup. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:187-194` (`pch(Var, int...)`) — `mapping.length` is dereferenced on line 190 before the `mapping != null` check on line 193; the null check is dead. Also when `mapping.length == 0` every row falls back to the raw index `i`, silently ignoring the (empty) mapping. [nit] Fix: drop the null check, validate `mapping.length > 0` up front.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:135-143,156-164` (`color(Var)`, `fill(Var)`) — the colour array is built eagerly inside the supplier every time `getColor(row)` is called (`getParamValue` re-applies the function on every access), so a `Points` artist with n points and a `color(Var)` option does O(n²) work and allocates n arrays of n `Color`s during one paint. Same for `sz(Var, factor, offset)` which re-streams the whole `Var` into a new `VarDouble` per `getSz(row)` call. [bug] Fix: memoise the computed value inside `NamedParam` (compute once per bind) or make suppliers cache.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:124-128,145-149` — `color(-1)`/`fill(-1)` is a magic sentinel meaning "no colour"; not documented anywhere and only works when exactly one index is given (`color(1, -1)` calls `getColor(-1)` which wraps to the last palette colour). [inconsistency] Fix: add explicit `color.none()`/`fill.none()` factories.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:371-393` (`getColor`, `getFill`, `getSz`, `getPch`) — `row % value.length` throws `ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException` for negative rows and `ArithmeticException` when a user supplies an empty array (`color(new Color[0])`, `labels()` with no args is fine but `color()` with no args gives `Color[0]`). [bug] Fix: validate non-empty in the factories, use `Math.floorMod`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:366-453` — every getter is an unchecked cast `(Palette) getParamValue(_palette)`; `NamedParam` carries `V` but `NamedParamSet.getParamValue` erases it to `Object`. A `GOpt<?>` with a colliding name but a different type (possible because `GOpt(GOpt<?> p, fun)` copies only the *name*) produces a `ClassCastException` at paint time far from the cause. [design] Fix: make `getParamValue` generic `<V> V getParamValue(NamedParam<S, V>)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:81` — default font is `new Font("DejaVu Sans", PLAIN, 20)` while `Plot`/`GridLayer` hard-code `"Verdana"`. Two different default font families; both fall back to a platform logical font when absent, which makes text rendering (and image-comparison tests) machine dependent. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:62` — `_palette` default supplier allocates a fresh `Palette.tableau21()` (21 `Color` objects) on every `getPalette()` call, which happens once per `getColor(i)` call inside `color(int...)`. [nit] Fix: constant palettes should be singletons.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:86,197,263,301,315` — options are exposed as lowercase nested *interfaces* (`palette.hue()`, `pch.circleWire()`, `sort.asc()`, `halign.left()`) used as namespaces, while others are plain static methods (`color(...)`, `lwd(...)`), and `bins`, `prob`, `stacked`, `points`, `top` take raw primitives. Mixed API style; the nested-interface trick also puts three inconsistently named things (`hAlign` field, `halign` interface, `HALIGN_*` constants) next to each other. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:76-77,285-299` — `widths(double...)` vs `widths(int...)` overloads switch semantics (relative vs absolute) purely on literal type: `widths(1, 2)` is absolute pixels, `widths(1.0, 2.0)` is relative fractions. Easy to misuse silently. [design] Fix: `widthsAbs(int...)` / `widthsRel(double...)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/GOpts.java:36` — `GOpts` is not `Serializable` (neither is `NamedParamSet`), yet `Artist`, `Plot`, `GridLayer` all implement `Serializable` and hold a `GOpts` field. Serialising any `Plot` throws `NotSerializableException`. The `@Serial serialVersionUID` declarations across the package are therefore decorative. [bug] Fix: either make `NamedParamSet` serializable (the `SFunction` suppliers already are) or drop `Serializable` from the figure hierarchy.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/printer/opt/NamedParamSet.java:58-79` (used by GOpts) — `register` walks `clazz.getDeclaredFields()` with reflection on every root `GOpts` construction (`new GOpts()` in every artist constructor). That is dozens of reflective field reads per artist. [design] Fix: register once per class in a static map.

##### Sizes

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Sizes.java:33,67-69` — `System.arraycopy(relativeSizes, 0, dimensions, 0, relativeSizes.length)` and the absolute loop both throw `ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException` when the user passes more sizes than there are rows/cols (`widths(1,1,1)` on a 2-column grid). [bug] Fix: copy `min(length, sizes.length)` or throw a descriptive `IllegalArgumentException`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Sizes.java:43-45` — when some cells are expandable (`-1`) and the fixed fractions already sum to ≥ 1, *all* dimensions become 0, so every plot in the grid gets a 0×0 rectangle and `Plot.buildViewport` produces negative widths (later divisions by zero/negatives in `xScale`). Same at line 79-81 when absolute sizes exceed the total. Silent blank output. [bug] Fix: throw, or normalise fixed fractions and give expandable cells 0.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Sizes.java:82` — `(totalSize - sum) / countExpandable` divides by 0 when no cell is expandable; result is `Infinity` but unused, so it works by accident. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Sizes.java:29` — record exposes mutable `double[]`/`int[]` components and one of the two arrays is always `null`; a sealed interface `Relative(double[])`/`Absolute(int[])` would remove the `absolute` flag and the null. [design]

##### Palette

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:412-413` (`MonoHueGradient` constructor) — arguments to `super(...)` are swapped: `super(new Range(minSat, maxSat), inputRange)` stores the saturation range as `inputRange` and the data range as `internalRange`. `getColor` then normalises the data value against the saturation range and maps the proportion back into the *data* range as a saturation, producing out-of-[0,1] saturations for any realistic data range. [bug] Fix: `super(inputRange, new Range(minSat, maxSat))`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:385-390` (`BiColorGradient.getColor`) — interpolation is reversed: proportion 0 (value == range start) returns `end`, proportion 1 returns `start`. `bicolor(RED, BLUE)` yields blue at the low end. Same reversed formula in `RedBlueGradient` (line 247-251: value 0 → blue, 360 → red, despite the name "redBlue"). [bug] Fix: `start * (1-p) + end * p`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:244-252` (`RedBlueGradient`) — `value` is not clamped to `[0,360]`; any value > 360 or < 0 gives an RGB component outside 0..255 and `new Color(r,g,b,a)` throws `IllegalArgumentException`. `inputRange`/`internalRange` are stored but never used here, in `HueBlueRed` (line 229-231) or `RedGreenBluePalette` (line 266-272, throws for value < 0 or > 510). [bug] Fix: apply `inputRange.proportion` + `internalRange.fromProportion` + clamp like `GrayColorPalette` does.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:363-365,211-213` (`IndexedPalette`, `GrayColorPalette`) — `while (index < 0) index += n` is a loop over ~2^31/n iterations for `-Infinity` (cast to `Integer.MIN_VALUE`); `NaN` casts to 0 and silently maps to colour 0 (black). [bug] Fix: `Math.floorMod`, and treat NaN explicitly (transparent or throw).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:355-370` — `IndexedPalette` computes an `internalRange` of `[0, n-1]` but `getColor` ignores both ranges, so `Palette.gray(start,end)` respects the range while `tableau21()`/`standard()` do not. The abstract contract of `getColor(double)` is thus different per subclass. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:136-142` (`Range.proportion`) — `start == end` divides by zero and returns `NaN`, which then feeds `HSBtoRGB`/`(int)` casts with undefined colours. Also `proportion` skips clamping when the range is the NaN "empty" range, so `HueGradient` with `emptyRange` passes raw data through as a fraction. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:93-95` — `hue(double hueStart, double hueEnd)` forwards its arguments as `rangeFrom, rangeTo` (the *data* range) to the 4-arg version whose first two parameters are the hue range; parameter names contradict behaviour. `GOpts.palette.hue(start, end)` inherits the confusion. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:105-115` — `RedRGB()`, `GreenRGB()`, `BlueRGB()` are capitalised factory methods next to `redBlue()`, `hueBlueRed()`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:161,278,300,332` — four different palette classes share the same `serialVersionUID = -7776446499900459739L` (copy-paste); `IndexedPalette`, `BiColorGradient`, `HueGradient`, `MonoHueGradient`, `RedRGB`… have none although `Palette` is `Serializable`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/Palette.java:130-155` — `Range` is a `protected record` inside a public abstract class but is used by package-private subclasses only; `emptyRange` is a shared static instance used as a sentinel via `NaN`. An `Optional`/nullable range or a boolean would be clearer. [design]

##### PchPalette

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/PchPalette.java:99-105,145-160,195-209` — all `*_FILL` drawers call `g2d.setColor(fill)` where `fill` is the option default `null`. `SunGraphics2D.setColor(null)` is a silent no-op, so a filled marker with no `fill(...)` option is painted with whatever colour was last set (previous point's colour, axis black, …). `Points.paint` line 103 pre-sets `getFill(i)` (also null) so nothing rescues it. [bug] Fix: fall back to `color` when `fill == null`, or make the default fill non-null.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/PchPalette.java:125,127` — `g2d.fill(new Line2D.Double(...))` is a no-op (a line has no interior). Dead code. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/PchPalette.java:91-228` — every drawer sets stroke and colour and never restores them; the triangle/square path construction is copy-pasted three times each. A `Shape shape(x,y,sz)` per marker plus a generic wire/fill/full painter would remove ~120 lines. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/PchPalette.java:38` — `enum PchPalette implements Serializable` is redundant (enums are always serializable) and its `Mapping` field (`StandardPchPalette`) is not serializable anyway (enum constants serialise by name so it is harmless). [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/opt/PchPalette.java:133,148,164` — triangle is scaled by `1.2` while circle/square are not, so `sz` means different pixel extents per marker; not documented. [nit]

#### rapaio.graphics.base

##### XWilkinson

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:286` — `double step = z == -1 ? j * q : j * q * Math.pow(base, z);` special-cases `z == -1` to the *same* step as `z == 0`, so steps of `q/base` (0.1, 0.2, 0.5 for base 10) are never generated, and the `z == 0` candidate is scored twice. Ranges like `[0, 0.9]` cannot get 0.1/0.2 ticks and fall through to `z = -2` (0.01…) with fewer/uglier labels. This is not part of Wilkinson's algorithm. [bug] Fix: `j * q * Math.pow(base, z)` unconditionally.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:224` — `getFormattedValue` uses `NumberFormat.getInstance(Locale.getDefault())`; axis labels (and therefore rendered images) depend on the JVM default locale (`1,5` vs `1.5`, grouping separators). Makes tests non-portable. [bug] Fix: `Locale.ROOT` or a configurable formatter.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:291-292` — `(int) (Math.floor(dmax / step - (k - 1)) * j)` overflows silently for very large magnitudes (e.g. epoch-millisecond time axes ~1.7e12 with small steps) and the `for (start = min_start; start <= max_start; ...)` loop can run billions of iterations or not at all. [bug] Fix: use `long` and bound the loop.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:44` — `DEEFAULT_EPS` typo in a public constant. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:107` — `public boolean loose` is a mutable public field on an otherwise immutable object. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:191-193` — `legibility()` always returns 1 with a "Maybe later" comment; weights `w[3]` are dead. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:337-351` (`searchBounded`) — runs the full search `maxM-1` times and selects by *count of labels*, ignoring the Wilkinson score; `best.getList()` is recomputed (allocating) on every comparison. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:269,278,285` — `while (j < Integer.MAX_VALUE)` loops are `while(true)` in disguise; termination depends on the score pruning, which fails if `w` has a zero simplicity weight. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:74-104` — `forSeconds`, `forMinutes` … `forYears` factories are never used (time axes use `PrettyTimeInterval`). Dead code. [nit]

#### rapaio.graphics.plot

##### Axis

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:323-329,379-383` (`TypeTime.computeArtifacts`, `selectInstantMarkersByElimination`) — `instants.get(0)` / `instants.get(size-1)` are called *before* the `instants.isEmpty()` check on line 330, so an empty list from `getInstantList` throws `IndexOutOfBoundsException`. [bug] Fix: check emptiness first.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:316-317` — `Instant.ofEpochMilli((long) axis.min)` with `NaN` min (time axis with no data) silently becomes epoch 0 (1970) instead of skipping. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:401-408` (`TypeDiscreteTime.computeArtifacts`) — overwrites `axis.min = 0; axis.max = values.size()` *after* `Axis.computeArtifacts` applied hard limits and the ±0.5 single-value fix, so `xLim` is ignored for candlestick charts, and no tickers/labels are produced (discrete time axes have no labels at all). `computeInnerValues` also first collects epoch-millis for these instants into min/max, which is wasted. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:152-157` (`CategoryInterval.overlaps`) — `Math.abs(minEnd - maxBegin) > 1e-20` is true for *disjoint* intervals too (the sign is discarded). Currently unused, so dead and wrong. [bug] Fix: `minEnd > maxBegin`, or delete.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:134,184,214-243` — `Domain` keeps every numeric data value in a `HashSet<Double>` (boxed) and `computeInnerValues` copies them all again into a `TreeSet` and a `List` (`discreteValues`) that nobody reads. For a scatter of 1M points that is ~3 boxed copies of the data just to get min/max. [design] Fix: track running min/max; drop `discreteValues`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:143-150` (`hardLim`) — cannot clear a hard limit once set (`NaN` is ignored), `clear()`/`clearData()` keep them, and `hardMin > hardMax` is accepted (axis length negative → mirrored plot). [bug] Fix: validate ordering; accept NaN as "unset".
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:204-207` — `unionTime` and `instantValues` are never called (grep: only `unionDiscreteTime` is used, by `CandlestickChart`); `Points`/`Lines` with `INSTANT` vars go through `unionNumeric` with epoch millis. Dead path. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:80-83` — `min == max` widened by ±0.5 regardless of magnitude (for a value of 1e9 that is a 5e-10 relative range; for epoch millis it is half a millisecond). [nit] Fix: widen relative to magnitude (`max(0.5, |v|*1e-3)`).
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:88` — `plot.thickerMinSpace` / `THICKER_PAD` / `leftThick` — "thicker" is used throughout for "tick(er)". [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:297` (`TypeCategory`) — iterates a `HashMap` so label order follows hash order; harmless for painting but makes `toString()`/tests order-dependent on JDK hashing. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java:275,283,294,304,401` — every `Type*` subclass repeats `implements Serializable` although `Type` already does. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Axis.java` (whole class) — no log-scale support at all; the "log scale with non-positive values" concern from the brief does not apply because scaling is strictly linear (`Plot.xScale`). Worth stating as a limitation rather than a bug. [design]

##### Plot

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:116` — `this.options.bind(opts);` discards the returned child `GOpts` (`bind` returns `new GOpts(this).apply(...)`), so options passed to `new Plot(opts)` / `Plotter.plot(opts)` are silently ignored. `GridLayer` (line 73) correctly uses `new GOpts().apply(options)`. [bug] Fix: `this.options.apply(opts)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:186` (`yUnscale`) — formula `(viewport.y - y) * len / height + 1. + min` should add `len`, not `1.` (inverse of `yScale`). Wrong for any axis whose length ≠ 1. Only reachable via `Artist.yUnscale`, which has no callers today. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:338-342` — `add()` calls `pc.bind(this)` and then `addArtist(pc)` which calls `bind` again; artists with side effects in `bind` (e.g. `Histogram.bind` rebinding options) run twice. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:78-80` — fonts are `protected static final` hard-coded `"Verdana"`; not configurable per plot, ignore `GOpts.font`, and are platform dependent (Verdana is not on Linux by default). [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:203-296` (`paint`) — no clip is set to the viewport before artists paint, and several artists (Histogram bars below `yLim`, ABLine, Segment, Text, FunLine) can draw outside the axes area over the tick labels. [bug] Fix: `g2d.setClip(viewport)` (or paint each artist on `g2d.create()` with clip) before line 293.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:238-242,250-254` — rotation is undone with `rotate(+π/2); translate(-xx,-yy)` instead of saving/restoring the `AffineTransform`; floating-point round-off accumulates across labels. [nit] Fix: `AffineTransform saved = g2d.getTransform(); … g2d.setTransform(saved)`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:150-175` — `buildViewport` never guards against small rectangles: width/height go negative and `xScale` divides by a negative or zero `viewport.width`. `GridLayer` with many cells hits this. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:177-191` — `xScale`/`yScale` divide by `xAxis.length()`; with no data and no limits both min/max are `NaN`, so every artist coordinate is `NaN` and Java2D silently draws nothing (no error, blank plot). [nit] Fix: default axis to `[0,1]` when empty.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:356-552` vs `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/Plotter.java:71-212` — ~30 factory methods are duplicated between `Plot` (instance) and `Plotter` (static); the two lists have already drifted: `Plotter` has `points(Var y)` and `corrGram(...)`, `Plot` does not; `Plot` has `segmentLine/segmentArrow`, `Plotter` does not. [inconsistency] Fix: `Plotter.x(...)` = `plot().x(...)` generated, or drop one API.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:88` — `final GOpts options` is package-private and mutated from `GridLayer.add` via `setParent`; `Artist.bind` also re-parents. The parent chain is therefore rebuilt as a side effect of `add` order, and re-adding a plot to a second grid silently moves its option parent. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:504-516` (`qqplot`) — mutates title/labels unconditionally, overriding anything the user set before, like all artist `bind` overrides below. [inconsistency]

##### Artist

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Artist.java:76-89` (`union`) — `(x finite, y NaN)` adds x only, but `(x infinite, y finite)` adds nothing; NaN and ±Infinity are treated differently without reason. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Artist.java:37-46` (`bind`) — the first artist decides the axis type; a later artist with a different type (e.g. `BarPlot` category x after `Points` numeric x) is silently accepted and mixes coordinate systems. [design] Fix: throw on type mismatch.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Artist.java:34-35` — `options` and `plot` are non-final protected fields; every subclass must remember to initialise `options` in its constructor (they all do, in 4 slightly different styles: `new GOpts().apply(opts)`, `.apply(fill(7)).apply(opts)`, `.apply(bins(10)).apply(opts)`, and `options = ...` without `this.`). [inconsistency] Fix: `Artist(GOpt<?>... opts)` constructor taking defaults.

##### GridLayer

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/GridLayer.java:144-158,161-186` — `prepare`/`paint` lay out children against the raw rectangle `r`, not the computed `viewport`; `buildViewport` (MINIMUM_PAD, title space, `sizeTitle`) is computed and then ignored, so a grid title is drawn on top of the first row of plots. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/GridLayer.java:106-116` (`add(Plot)`) — when every cell is already assigned the plot is silently dropped (only `setParent` runs). [bug] Fix: throw `IllegalStateException("grid is full")`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/GridLayer.java:131-141` — no bounds/overlap check on `add(row, col, w, h, plot)`: out-of-range gives a bare `ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException`, overlapping cells silently overwrite `assign` while both plots stay in `list` and are painted on top of each other. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/GridLayer.java:185` — `g2d.create()` per child is never `dispose()`d. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/GridLayer.java:127` — `add(int x, int y, Plot)` parameter names `x, y` map to `row, col` in the delegate; inverted mental model relative to screen x/y. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/GridLayer.java:148-156,176-184` — layout computation is duplicated between `prepare` and `paint`; `sizeTitle` is never reset if `title(null)` is called after a first `prepare`. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/GridLayer.java:50-52` — `MARKERS_FONT`, `LABELS_FONT` are unused copies of `Plot`'s constants. [nit]

##### Plotter

#### plot/artist — Points, Lines, Legend

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Points.java:63-65` (`yAxisType`) — tests `x.type()` instead of `y.type()` (copy-paste); a numeric-x / instant-y scatter gets a numeric y axis, and an instant-x / numeric-y one gets a *time* y axis. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Points.java:103-105` — sets colour, stroke and `Composite` (alpha) on the shared `Graphics2D` per point and never restores; every artist painted afterwards inherits the alpha. `Histogram`, `DensityLine`, `FunLine` do save/restore the composite, `Points`, `BoxPlot`, `Lines` do not. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Points.java:68-72` (`bind`) — unconditionally overwrites the plot's `xLab`/`yLab` with variable names, clobbering labels the user set earlier in the fluent chain (`Plotter.scatters` works around it with `.xLab(null)` afterwards). `Lines` does not do this; `Histogram`/`Histogram2D` also force `leftThick/leftMarkers/...` back to true. Artists should not mutate plot presentation; at most set a label when it is still `null`. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Points.java:103,107-108` — `g2d.setColor(options.getFill(i))` is redundant (the drawer sets its own colours) and the default `fill` is `null`, so `pch` `*_FILL` shapes are filled with a stale colour (see `PchPalette` item). Default fill should be the colour, or the drawer should skip fill when null. [bug]

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Lines.java:56-58` — rows with a missing coordinate are removed in the constructor (`stream().complete()`), so a line is drawn *across* gaps instead of breaking at NaN (what Points/most libraries do). - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Lines.java:52` — `Lines(Var y)` does not sort by x and `Lines(x, y)` does not either; a scatter-ordered x produces a spaghetti line. Document or sort. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Lines.java:88-93` — stroke is set before the loop and again on every iteration; `setBackground` is pointless. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Lines.java:99-106,218` — `Clip.lineClip` returns a *segment* packed into a `Rectangle2D.Double(x0, y0, x1-x0, y1-y0)` with possibly negative width/height and the caller reads `getMinX()/getMaxX()`; this only works because `RectangularShape.getMinX()` returns `getX()` unnormalised. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Lines.java` — `Lines` implements Cohen–Sutherland clipping itself while `Points`, `DensityLine`, `FunLine` use `contains` (drop segment), `Histogram` drops bars, `BarPlot`/`BoxPlot`/`Text`/`Segment` do not clip at all. Setting `g2d.setClip(viewport)` once in `Plot.paint` would replace all of this and stop artists drawing over the axes. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Legend.java:89,92` — measures text with `g2d.getFontMetrics()` (whatever font `Plot.paint` left set: `LABELS_FONT` when `xLabel != null`, `MARKERS_FONT` otherwise) and never applies `options.getFont()`; legend row height changes depending on whether the plot has an x label. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Legend.java:56,65` — eagerly builds a 255-colour `fill` array by calling `options.getPalette()` 255 times (each call allocates a fresh default palette), and does so in both constructors (duplicated). Compute lazily / once. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Legend.java:45,96-101` — `place` supports only `UP_LEFT (0)`; any other value silently draws at data coordinates `(-1, -1)`. Either implement the other corners or validate. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Legend.java:104-105` — colour swatch is a `draw`n 1-pixel-high `Rectangle2D`, ignoring `lwd`/`pch`, so a legend for a `Points` series cannot show the marker; default `labels` is `{""}` so a legend with no labels draws one empty row. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Legend.java:112-114` — `updateDataRange` is empty, so a `Legend` (or `FunLine`, `Text`, `Segment`) alone on a plot yields NaN axes and nothing is drawn (see `Axis.computeArtifacts`). [nit]

#### plot/artist — Histogram, Histogram2D, BarPlot

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:152-167,185-188` — the last bar is culled when `binStart(bins) = min + (max-min)/bins*bins` rounds above `hist.max()` (`maxBin > xAxis.max()`); with random `(min, max, bins)` this happens in about 2 % of cases (quick simulation: 426/20000). Use `i == bins ? max : ...` for the last edge, or compare with a tolerance. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:156,165` — with a user `xLim` narrower than the data, bars that only *partially* fall outside are dropped entirely rather than clipped; bars are also drawn below `yAxis.min()` when `yLim` starts above 0 (`yScale(0)` not clamped). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:111-114` (`buildData`) — density normalisation divides by `sum * step`; for an all-missing variable `sum == 0` and for a constant variable `step == 0` the result is NaN/Inf and is unioned into the axis domain (`allowUnion` rejects non-finite, so silently ignored). No error surfaces. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:76,79,102-104` — `HistogramTable` is built in the constructor with `options.getBins()` (default 30) *before* `bind`, so the `bins == -1 → hist.bins()` rebinding in `bind` only applies if the user passed `bins(-1)`; then `options.bind` creates a child options whose parent is later reset by `super.bind`... order-dependent and confusing. Compute bins once in the constructor. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:139` — stroke is set once and never restored (composite is, per bar). [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram2D.java:124-125` — `options.getFill(0)` is `null` by default (no default fill applied in the constructor, unlike `Histogram`'s `fill(7)`), so `c.getRed()` throws `NullPointerException` unless the user passes `fill(...)`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram2D.java:89-91` — bin index is clamped from above (`min(bins-1, ...)`) but not from below; with `xLim`/`yLim` tighter than the data, points below the axis minimum give a negative index → `ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException`. Also uses the *axis* range (including other artists) as the histogram range, so bin edges move when another artist is added. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram2D.java:49,92-94` — `maxFreq` is a field never reset in `computeData`; harmless now (same data) but wrong if `paint` is ever called with a changed range. `int blue` is actually the alpha channel. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/BarPlot.java:196-243` — vertices are `(int)`-truncated and drawn with `fillPolygon/drawPolygon` (5-element arrays, `nPoints = 4`), while every other artist uses `Rectangle2D.Double`/`Line2D.Double` with sub-pixel coordinates; ignores `lwd` and `alpha`. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/BarPlot.java:57-58,66-67` — `INT` is accepted as a category type but the error message says "nominal only"; `categories` are ordered by first appearance, not by the variable's level order, and `Var.levels()` is never consulted, so two barplots of the same nominal var with different row order produce different x axes (and `unionCategory` throws when they share a plot). [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/BarPlot.java:125-131` — categories whose total is exactly `0.0` are dropped, which also removes categories whose positive and negative values cancel; stacked drawing (`ystart += hits`) is wrong for negative values. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/BarPlot.java:82-83` — magic `shift = 9` palette offset for default fills; `horizontal` option (supported by `Histogram`) is ignored. [nit]

#### plot/artist — BoxPlot, DensityLine, FunLine, ABLine

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/ABLine.java:101` — `g2d.setColor(options.getFill(0))` uses *fill* (default `null` → `setColor` ignored → stale colour) although the constructor sets a default via `color(Color.LIGHT_GRAY)` and users pass `color(...)`. Should be `getColor(0)`. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/ABLine.java:114` — `y1 = yScale(plot.xAxis().min())` should be `yScale(plot.yAxis().min())`. Additionally the fallback assumes a positive slope (left end clipped at `yAxis.min`, right end at `yAxis.max`); for `a < 0` the intersections are swapped and for `a == 0` it divides by zero. Clip the line against the viewport rectangle instead. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/ABLine.java:89-97` — `hLine`/`vLine` union their coordinate into the axis domain, so `vLine(1000)` on data in `[0,1]` stretches the x axis; R's `abline` does not. Debatable, document. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/ABLine.java:63-68` — the h/v constructor stores `b = a` and uses two booleans `h`/`v` where an enum (`HORIZONTAL/VERTICAL/SLOPE`) would be clearer. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/BoxPlot.java:66-70` — the `(Var x, Var factor)` constructor does not apply the defaults (`pch(wrap(0,3)), color(0), fill(240,240,240)`) that the other two constructors apply, so grouped boxplots render differently (no box fill, default pch) from ungrouped ones. [inconsistency]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/BoxPlot.java:67` — missing values of `x` are not filtered (`x.stream()` not `.complete()`) so NaN enters `Quantiles`; `BoxPlot(Frame)` (line 85) does not filter non-numeric columns, so a nominal column yields NaN quantiles and invisible boxes. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/BoxPlot.java:117` — `setComposite` with alpha is never restored (leaks into later artists). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/BoxPlot.java:159-182` — fence tests are asymmetric: upper whisker requires `point < q3 + innerFence` (strict) while the lower requires `point >= q1 - innerFence`; a point exactly on the upper inner fence is neither whisker nor outlier and simply disappears. Variable `lowerqhisker` is a typo. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/BoxPlot.java:165,174` — outlier markers use `options.getPch(1)`/`getPch(0)` as *pch indices* while `getSz(i)`/`getColor(i)` use the variable index `i`; the contract of the per-row option lookup is different in the same call. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/DensityLine.java:85-90` (`updateDataRange`) — calls `kde.pdf(x)` (O(n)) for every observation → O(n²) on every `prepare`; for 50k points this is seconds per repaint, only to find the y max. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/DensityLine.java:118-125` — the fill polygon starts at the first grid point, skips any segment whose endpoints are not `contains`, then closes back to the first point; with a `yLim`, gaps produce slanted chords across the excluded region instead of clipping to the axis. - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/DensityLine.java:94-95` vs `FunLine.java:53` — `DensityLine` samples `GOpts.points` (default 256) while `FunLine` overrides the default to 1024; `Var`s are allocated just to hold two `double[]`. [inconsistency]

#### rapaio.graphics.plot.artist

##### Cross-cutting (all artists)

- `Points.java:103-108`, `BoxPlot.java:136`, `ABLine.java:101`, `Segment.java:87`, `ROCCurve.java:79`, `Legend.java:104`, `Histogram2D.java:124` (all under `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/`) — the default `fill` option is `null` and `Graphics2D.setColor(null)` is a silent no-op in `SunGraphics2D`. Every artist that does `g2d.setColor(options.getFill(i))` therefore paints with *whatever colour was set last* (axis black, previous artist's colour) unless the user passes `fill(...)`. Concretely: `hLine(1)`/`vLine(1)` ignore their `color(LIGHT_GRAY)` default and the user's `color(...)`; `segmentLine(...)` ignores `color(...)`; `BoxPlot(x, factor)` boxes are filled black (see below); `Points` with `pch.circleFill()` and no fill uses a stale colour. [bug] Fix: `Color fill = options.getFill(i); if (fill == null) fill = options.getColor(i);` centrally in `GOpts.getFill`, or give `_fill` a non-null default.
- `Points.java:105`, `BoxPlot.java:117` — `g2d.setComposite(AlphaComposite...)` is set and **never restored** (Histogram, FunLine, DensityLine, Segment, Matrix, Candlestick do restore). Everything painted after a `Points` or `BoxPlot` artist (legend, text, other artists) inherits its alpha. [bug]
- `Text.java:97` — `g2d.setClip(new Rectangle(plot.getViewport()))` inside the per-line loop, never restored, and `setClip` *replaces* any outer clip instead of intersecting (`clip()`). Every artist painted after a `Text` is clipped; artists before it are not. Clipping should be done once in `Plot.paint`. [bug]
- All artists set `stroke`/`font`/`color` without save/restore, and `Plot.paint` relies on `g2d.setFont(MARKERS_FONT)` state surviving; `Legend.paint:89` and `CorrGram.paint:119` measure text with `g2d.getFontMetrics()` (current font), so their layout depends on which artist painted before them (e.g. a `Text` artist with a 20pt font). [bug] Fix: `Plot.paint` should hand each artist `g2d.create()` and `dispose()` it, or artists should save/restore.
- Constructors initialise options in four styles (`this.options = new GOpts().apply(opts)`, `options = new GOpts().apply(opts)`, `.apply(defaults).apply(opts)`, `Legend`'s two-step `options = options.apply(...)`). `CandlestickChart` has no `serialVersionUID` while every other artist does. [inconsistency]
- `Histogram.bind`, `Histogram2D.bind`, `Points.bind`, `ROCCurve.bind`, `CorrGram.bind`, `Plot.qqplot` unconditionally overwrite `xLab`/`yLab`/tick flags set by the user earlier in the fluent chain (`plot().xLab("x").hist(v)` loses the label). [inconsistency] Fix: only set when null.
- Default `points` differs: `FunLine` forces 1024, `DensityLine` inherits 256; default `bins` differs: `Histogram` 30 (GOpts), `Histogram2D` 10. [inconsistency]
- `BarPlot`, `BoxPlot` build their geometry with `(int)` casts and `fillPolygon`/`drawPolygon`, `Histogram`/`Candlestick` use `Rectangle2D.Double`: sub-pixel alignment differs between bar-like artists in the same plot. [nit]

##### Histogram

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:156-158,165-167` — a bin is skipped entirely when *any* part of it lies outside the axis limits (`maxBin > max || minBin < min`). With `xLim` not aligned to bin edges the first and last visible bins vanish instead of being clipped. [bug] Fix: clamp the bin rectangle to the axis range.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:168-172` — `mind = min(d, yAxis.max())` clamps the top but bars always start at `yScale(0)`; with `yLim(1, …)` bars are drawn below the axis over the tick labels (no clip in `Plot.paint`). Horizontal variant same at 159-163. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:112-113` — density normalisation `step = (max-min)/bins` is 0 for a constant variable (single distinct value, or `hist(v, a, a)`), giving `Infinity`/`NaN` densities and NaN axis ranges. [bug] Fix: guard `step == 0`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:102-104` — `if (options.getBins() == -1)` can only be true if the user explicitly passes `bins(-1)` (default is 30), and the `HistogramTable` has already been built with the original value in the constructor; rebinding is cosmetic. Also runs twice because `Plot.add` binds twice. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Histogram.java:142-143,181` — `getComposite`/`setComposite` per bar inside the loop. [nit]

##### Lines

Also `.complete()` drops rows with a missing value in either var and then *connects across the gap*, so a missing value does not break the polyline (unlike most plotting libraries; `Points` treats missing as skipped). - `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/CandlestickChart.java:58-62,213-219` — comment says state `-1` is red but the code uses index `2`; `Stroke` objects with the odd dash array `{2f, 0f, 2f}` are allocated inside the loop; the local `lwd` shadows the meaning of the `lwd` option (it is a pixel width here); `bars` list is not defensively copied. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:86-97` (`isOutside`) — (1) compares *pixel* coordinates (`fillPoints` are already `xScale`d) against *data* axis limits; (2) the max tests use `<` instead of `>` (`x < xAxis.max()`), so with a hard `xLim`/`yLim` set every polygon inside the range is treated as outside and the whole iso plot vanishes. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:324-332` (`MeshStripe`) — a NaN grid value is neither `< low` nor `> high` and is classified as side `1` (inside the band), so missing cells are filled as if they held a mid-band value. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:114` — band colour is `options.getPalette().getColor(levelMidpoint)` with the *default* indexed Tableau palette, which floors the level value to an index: for levels inside `[0,1)` every band is black. `Plotter.isoBands` sets no gradient default; either default to `Palette.hue(...)` over `[levels[0], levels[n-1]]` or document. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:161-165` — `visitEach` rebuilds a `MeshStripe` (full grid classification) per level *and* is called twice (fill pass and contour pass), so a paint costs `2·(levels-1)` grid scans. Build all stripes once. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:60-62,163` — `levels` is not validated (must be sorted ascending, length ≥ 2; otherwise nothing is drawn silently); the array is stored by reference. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:139-149` — magic constants `+12`, `< 3/6/9`, `lines > 1` for deciding which polygon edges are true contour segments are undocumented; saddle cells (`0202`, `2020`, `0212`...) are not disambiguated, producing wrong connections in the classic marching-squares ambiguous cases. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:169-174` — cells whose corner lies outside the axis are skipped whole (no clipping), leaving a jagged margin when `xLim`/`yLim` cut through the grid. [nit]

#### Cross-cutting design

- Option system (`GOpt<V>`/`GOpts`): varargs of `GOpt<?>` give no compile-time check that an option applies to an artist (`bins(10)` on `points(...)` is accepted and ignored). Getters cast from `Object`. The `int...` vs `Color...` vs `Var` overloads plus the `-1` sentinel make `color`/`fill` semantics hard to predict. A typed per-artist builder (or at least `GOpt<V>` + generic `getParamValue`) would remove the casts and the silent no-ops. [design]
- Every `Artist` constructor calls `new GOpts()`, which runs `NamedParamSet.register` by reflection over `GOpts` static fields; ~25 reflective field reads per artist, per construction (cache the default map statically). [design]
- `Graphics2D` hygiene: no artist or `Plot.paint` uses `create()/dispose()` or save/restore consistently — composite is restored by 7 artists and leaked by 4, stroke/colour/font/clip are never restored. A single `Graphics2D g = (Graphics2D) g2d.create(); g.setClip(viewport); try { artist.paint(g) } finally { g.dispose() }` loop in `Plot.paint` fixes state leaks, clipping and the ad-hoc `contains` culling at once. [design]
- Thread-safety: no mutable static state (palettes/`PchPalette` are stateless), but `Axis.Domain` and `Artist.options` are mutated during `prepare`, so a `Plot` instance must not be prepared/painted concurrently (e.g. two sizes at once). Not documented. [design]
- Coupling: `rapaio.graphics` depends on `java.desktop` (`Graphics2D`, `Font`, `BufferedImage`) all the way down to `GOpts` defaults and `Palette` (`java.awt.Color`), so the option/palette layer cannot be reused headless-without-AWT or tested without a graphics environment. [design]

#### Tests (`tst/rapaio/graphics`)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/AbstractArtistTest.java:43` — `ROOT = "/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-core/tst"` is a developer-machine absolute path (and names a different module); `regenerate()` is `final` and returns `false`, so the regeneration branch is unreachable dead code. Use a system property and the test resources dir. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/AbstractArtistTest.java:91-99` — the oracle is "90th percentile of per-channel absolute difference ≤ 45": since ≥90 % of an 800×600 plot is white background, a plot that renders *nothing* (or renders the wrong thing) usually still passes. It also masks the font/locale/timezone non-determinism noted above, and it hides real regressions (e.g. `Silhouette` width bug, `ABLine` colour bug). Use a stricter metric (mean abs diff, or count of pixels differing > threshold) and a small tolerance for AA differences. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/AbstractArtistTest.java:76-77` — `new Color(img.getRGB(x, y))` discards alpha (constructor without `hasAlpha`), so the alpha channel comparison is always 255 vs 255; 1.9 M boxed doubles are appended to `VarDouble`s per comparison (slow). [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinsonTest.java:40-57` — `testLocale` asserts en-US formatting *before* setting a locale (fails on a machine whose default locale uses `,`), and `Locale.setDefault` is not in `try/finally` so a failure leaks the `ru-RU` locale into other tests. `baseTest` (lines 60-140) has zero assertions, prints object hashes (`Labels` has no `toString`) and uses `LocalTime.now()`. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/LegendBugTest.java:54` — only `assertDoesNotThrow`; no assertion on legend content/position. [test]
- Coverage gaps (no test at all): `PolyLine`, `PolyFill`, `PolyPath`, `Legend` rendering, `Plotter.scatters`, `Plot(GOpt...)` option propagation (would have caught the `bind` bug), `Plot.yUnscale`, `Axis.TypeTime`/`TypeDiscreteTime` label generation, `GOpts`/`Sizes`/`Palette` unit tests (`Sizes` with more sizes than cells, `Palette` out-of-range values, `BiColorGradient` direction), `XWilkinson.search` termination on large offsets, empty/all-missing/single-value inputs for every artist, `Histogram2D` without `fill`, `IsoCurves` with `xLim`. [test]
- Determinism: all image tests depend on `Verdana`/`DejaVu Sans` being installed, on `Locale.getDefault()` (axis numbers) and on the default `TimeZone` (time axis); they also depend on the JDK's AA rasteriser (Marlin changes between releases). Pin a bundled font, `Locale.ROOT`, `TimeZone UTC` in `@BeforeAll`, and consider tolerance per pixel rather than percentile. [test]

#### Summary (top 5)

1. `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinson.java:291-294` — `search()` never terminates for large-offset/small-range axes (`int` overflow of `min_start/max_start`, reproduced with `search(1e12, 1e12+1, 5)`); any numeric axis with such data hangs the process. Also `simplicity` off-by-one, wrong `Q` set and the `z == -1` special case make tick choice systematically odd.
2. `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/Plot.java:116` — `this.options.bind(opts)` discards the returned child set, so options passed to `Plot(...)`/`Plotter.plot(...)` are ignored; `Plot.java:186` `yUnscale` has a `+ 1.` where `+ length()` is meant.
3. Graphics2D state leaks and inconsistent clipping across artists (`Points`, `BoxPlot`, `Lines` leak alpha; `Text` leaks clip+font; `Plot.paint` leaks font; `GridLayer`/`Segment` never `dispose()` created contexts). One `create/setClip/dispose` wrapper in `Plot.paint` fixes all of them.
4. Wrong-geometry bugs that the lenient image oracle cannot see: `Silhouette.java:146` (width = coordinate), `CandlestickChart.java:166` (y = pixel difference), `ABLine.java:101,114` (fill instead of colour, `xAxis().min()` in a y position, positive-slope assumption), `IsoCurves.java:86-97` (pixel vs data + inverted `<`), `Histogram.java:152-167` (last bar culled by FP rounding ~2 % of the time), `Points.java:64` (`x.type()` for y axis), `BiColorGradient`/`MonoHueGradient` in `Palette.java` (reversed / swapped ranges).
5. Test oracle (`AbstractArtistTest.java:91-99`, 90th-percentile ≤ 45 on mostly-white images) plus hard-coded developer path, font/locale/timezone dependence, and zero-assertion `XWilkinsonTest.baseTest`: the suite mostly proves "does not throw". Tighten the metric, pin fonts/locale/TZ, and add unit tests for `Axis`, `Sizes`, `Palette`, `XWilkinson` edge cases and empty/NaN inputs.

##### Silhouette

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Silhouette.java:146` — horizontal mode uses `w = xScale(score)` (an absolute pixel *coordinate*) as the rectangle *width*; it should be `xScale(score) - xScale(0)`. Bars are too long by `viewport.x + offset` pixels. Vertical mode (line 152-153) computes the width correctly. [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Silhouette.java:146,153,157` — a negative silhouette score yields a negative width (horizontal) or height (vertical); `Rectangle2D.Double` with negative extent is empty and `fill`/`draw` paint nothing, so negative scores (the interesting ones) are invisible. [bug] Fix: normalise `x/y` to `min` and use `abs`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Silhouette.java:141-142,161` — composite get/set per instance (thousands of times). No `serialVersionUID` unlike siblings. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Silhouette.java:54-55` — default `fill`/`color` arrays are sized by number of *instances* but indexed by *cluster*. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/Silhouette.java:60-66` — axis type depends on the `horizontal` option evaluated at bind time; the artist-level `horizontal(true)` default is applied in the constructor so it works, but a plot-level `horizontal` (via `Plot`/`GridLayer` options) is invisible here because the artist default wins. [inconsistency]

##### PolyLine / PolyFill / BasePoly / PolyPath

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/PolyLine.java:54`, `PolyFill.java:55` — `x.getDouble(0)` on empty vars throws `IndexOutOfBoundsException`; no empty guard (other artists have one). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/PolyFill.java:62` — colour from `getFill(0)`, null by default → stale colour (see cross-cutting). [bug]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/PolyLine.java:40`, `PolyFill.java:40` — identical `serialVersionUID = 8507830080358347197L` in two classes. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/PolyLine.java:52-60`, `PolyFill.java:53-59` — path construction duplicated; belongs in `BasePoly`. NaN coordinates are passed straight into `Path2D` (Java2D silently produces garbage), while `BasePoly.updateDataRange` ignores them. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/PolyPath.java:27-51` — mutable builder returning its live internal `VarDouble`s via `getPath()`; `PolyFill(PolyPath)` therefore aliases a mutable object. [design]

##### IsoCurves

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:90,96` (`isOutside`) — the hard-max checks use `<` instead of `>`: `x < plot.xAxis().max()` / `y < plot.yAxis().max()`. With `xLim` or `yLim` set every point *inside* the range is classified outside and all fills and contours are skipped → blank plot. [bug] Fix: `x > max`, `y > max`.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:324-332` (`MeshStripe` constructor) — a `NaN` grid value is neither `< low` nor `> high`, so it becomes side `1` (inside the band) and NaN cells are filled as if they were in range. [bug] Fix: skip cells with NaN corners.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:60-62` — `levels` is used unsorted and unvalidated (`levels[l] > levels[l+1]` gives an empty band silently); the array is stored by reference. [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:54` — `serialVersionUID` is the same value as `Matrix`'s (`-642370269224702175L`). [nit]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:146,178,289` — marching-squares magic numbers (`12`, `3/6/9`, `2222`, `q % 3 == 0`) without a comment explaining the 12-slot corner/edge encoding; saddle cases (e.g. `0202`) are not disambiguated. [design]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/src/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/IsoCurves.java:124-125` — fill path is both `draw`n (1px stroke) and `fill`ed to hide seams; with `alpha < 1` the overlapping stroke darkens every cell edge. [nit]

#### Tests (`/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics`)

- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/AbstractArtistTest.java:43` — `ROOT = "/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-core/tst"` is a hard-coded developer path pointing at a *different module* (`rapaio-core`, tests live in `rapaio-lib`); `regenerate()` writes goldens to the wrong place on any other machine. [test] Fix: derive from `src/test/resources` or a system property.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/AbstractArtistTest.java:91-99` — the image oracle is "90th percentile of per-channel absolute difference ≤ 45": up to 10 % of all channel samples may differ by any amount and the rest by up to 45/255. A missing legend, a missing bar, or a shifted small artist passes. Conversely the metric is what makes the tests survive font/antialiasing differences between JDKs, so the suite is neither strict nor portable by design. [test] Fix: render text-free goldens with `ImageTools` hints pinned and a fixed bundled font, then compare exactly (or with a tiny tolerance) and add non-image unit tests for the geometry.
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/AbstractArtistTest.java:76-79` — `new Color(img.getRGB(x, y))` discards alpha (the `int` constructor forces 255), so the alpha channel comparison is dead. Boxing 800×600×4 samples into two `VarDouble`s per test (≈4 M `addDouble` calls) makes every artist test slow for no benefit. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/AbstractArtistTest.java:50-52,62` — `regenerate()` is `final` and returns `false`: regenerating goldens requires editing the source. `assertTrue(condition)` has no message; the quantile is only printed via `WS.printf`. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/artist/ABLineTest.java:40-47` — passes `fill(3)…fill(8)` to `hLine/vLine/abLine`, enshrining the bug that line colour comes from `fill` and `color(...)` is ignored. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinsonTest.java:45-48` — asserts `"0;2,000,000;…"` before switching locale, so the test assumes the JVM default locale is en-US-like; `Locale.setDefault` is global and not restored in a `finally`. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/base/XWilkinsonTest.java:59-140` — `baseTest` has its `@Test` commented out and consists of `System.out.println` calls with no assertions. Dead test code. [test]
- `/home/ati/work/rapaio/rapaio-lib/tst/rapaio/graphics/plot/LegendBugTest.java:54` — only `assertDoesNotThrow`; does not check the legend was placed or coloured. [test]
- Coverage gaps (no test at all): `Axis`/`Axis.Domain` (hard limits, empty data, NaN/Infinity, single value, time axis with empty instants), `Palette` gradient maths (would have caught the reversed `BiColorGradient` and swapped `MonoHueGradient`), `GOpts` (parent chain, per-row wrap-around, `color(-1)` sentinel), `Sizes` (over-length arrays, sum ≥ 1), `PchPalette`, `Plot.xScale/xUnscale` round-trip (would have caught `yUnscale`), `GridLayer` full-grid/out-of-range `add`, `Legend(int place)` with non-zero place, `IsoCurves` with `xLim/yLim` (would have caught `isOutside`), `Silhouette` with negative scores, `Histogram2D` without `fill`, empty-var inputs for every artist, `Plot(opts)` option propagation (would have caught the discarded `bind`). [test]
- Every artist test is one `@Test` rendering one composite image (21 tests for 27 artists+figures); a single pixel-metric assertion per artist gives no localisation when it fails. [test]

#### Summary (top 5)

1. **`Plot` constructor discards its options** (`Plot.java:116`, `options.bind(opts)` return value ignored) — every `plot(color(...))`/`Plotter.plot(opts)` option is silently lost. One-line fix.
2. **Null default `fill` + `setColor(null)` no-op** — `ABLine`, `Segment`, `PolyFill`, `BoxPlot(x,factor)`, `Points` filled markers, `ROCCurve`, `Legend` paint with a stale colour and ignore the `color` option; `Histogram2D` NPEs. Fix centrally in `GOpts.getFill` (fall back to `color`) and route line artists to `getColor`.
3. **Graphics2D state leaks across artists** — `Points`/`BoxPlot` never restore the alpha composite, `Text` leaves a `setClip`, fonts/strokes are never restored and `Legend`/`CorrGram` measure text with whatever font is current. `Plot.paint` should give each artist `g2d.create()` + `dispose()` and set the viewport clip once.
4. **Range/geometry bugs that silently blank or distort output**: `IsoCurves.isOutside` inverted `<`/`>` (blank with `xLim`), `Silhouette` horizontal width = absolute coordinate and negative scores invisible, `CandlestickChart` separator endpoint is a pixel difference, `ABLine` a·x+b clipping assumes positive slope and uses `xAxis.min` as a y value, `Histogram` drops partially visible bins, `Histogram2D` negative bin index with limits, `GridLayer` ignores its own viewport/title space, `Sizes` AIOOBE/zero sizes, `Palette` reversed `BiColorGradient` and swapped `MonoHueGradient` ranges, `XWilkinson` `z == -1` special case suppressing 0.1/0.2/0.5 steps.
5. **Test strategy**: image-comparison with a 90th-percentile ≤ 45 tolerance on platform-dependent fonts (`Verdana`, `DejaVu Sans`, `Locale.getDefault()` number formatting) is simultaneously too loose to catch the bugs above and too fragile to be portable; there are no unit tests for `Axis`, `Palette`, `GOpts`, `Sizes` or scale maths. Add geometry-level unit tests and pin fonts/locale before touching the goldens.

Notes on coverage. Sections 2.3 and 2.4 both cover `rule`, `bayes`, `knn`, `simple`, `meta` (two independent reviewers), and sections 2.5 and 2.6 both cover `ml.eval`, `ml.loss` and `ml.common` (two independent reviewers); where they agree the finding is more trustworthy. On the `"?"` level: reviewers disagree about whether `levels()` includes it. It does not (see theme 1); claims in Part 2 built on "index 0 is `?`" should be re-read with that in mind.
